*Items the lens missed, added by the skeptic:*

- Will U. & Berg E. 2007 'Brain wave synchronization and entrainment to periodic acoustic stimuli', Neurosci Lett 424:55-60 (doi:10.1016/j.neulet.2007.07.036) — the cleanest modern EEG demonstration of entrainment to drum/click pulses at 1-8 Hz; essential for the 4-5 Hz claim.
- Oohashi T. et al. 2002 'Electroencephalographic measurement of possession trance in the field', Clin Neurophysiol 113:435-445 — the only field EEG of indigenous (Balinese kerauhan) possession trance (theta/alpha power increase); directly relevant to Balinese section.
- Huels E.R. et al. 2021 'Neural Correlates of the Shamanic State of Consciousness', Front Hum Neurosci 15:610466 (EEG of experienced practitioners during drumming) and Huels 2025 Front Psychol single-case cardiac study; Rogerson et al. 2021 Brain Sci 11:497 (EEG of a South African sangoma trance).
- Konopacki M. & Madison G. 2018 'EEG responses to shamanic drumming: does the suggestion of trance state moderate the strength of frequency components?' — a direct test of the expectation-vs-driving question.
- Woodside L., Kumar V.K. & Pekala R. 1997 'Monotonous percussion drumming and trance postures: a controlled evaluation of phenomenological effects', Anthropology of Consciousness 8(2-3):69-87, and Felicitas Goodman 1990 'Where the Spirits Ride the Wind' — the rattle-at-~210 bpm/15-min 'ritual body posture' protocol, the other major modern standard besides FSS.
- Szabó C. 2004/2006 'The effects of monotonous drumming on subjective experiences' (Music Therapy Today) and Kjellgren A. & Eriksson A. 2010 'Altered states during shamanic drumming: a phenomenological study', Int J Transpersonal Studies 29(2) — replications of the subjective effects.
- Rock A.J. et al. 2005-2008 (Rock, Baynes & Casey 2005; Rock, Abbott & Kambouropoulos 2008) — controlled studies of 'shamanic-like' journeying imagery with drumming at ~8 bps; Marie N. et al. 2024 'Scoping review on shamanistic trance practices', BMC Complement Med Ther.
- Wright P.A. 1991 'Rhythmic drumming in contemporary shamanism and its relationship to auditory driving and risk of seizure precipitation in epileptics', Anthropology of Consciousness 2(3-4):7-14 — the safety reference for the epilepsy caution.
- Walter V.J. & Walter W.G. 1949 'The central effects of rhythmic sensory stimulation', EEG Clin Neurophysiol 1:57-86 — origin of the 'driving' concept Neher borrowed; and Rouget's specific critique of Neher (Music and Trance, pp. 172-176).
- Becker J. 2004 'Deep Listeners: Music, Emotion, and Trancing' (Indiana UP) and Penman & Becker 2009 — the standard post-Rouget theory of trancing as a learned 'habitus of listening'; Bourguignon E. 1973 'Religion, Altered States of Consciousness and Social Change' (437 of 488 societies with institutionalised ASC); Needham R. 1967 'Percussion and transition', Man 2(4):606-614.
- Winkelman M. 2000/2010 'Shamanism: A Biopsychosocial Paradigm of Consciousness and Healing' — the main neuro-theoretical model ('integrative mode of consciousness') the project's framing implicitly relies on.
- Shona mbira dzavadzimu bira ceremonies (Berliner P. 1978 'The Soul of Mbira'; hosho rattles, 12-pulse cycles, ancestral spirit possession) — the best-documented African instrument-based possession tradition and a conspicuous omission.
- Tumbuka vimbuza (Malawi; UNESCO 2008): Friedson S. 1996 'Dancing Prophets: Musical Experience in Tumbuka Healing' — the key ethnomusicological study of drum-driven trance, with explicit 3:2 cross-rhythm analysis.
- Temiar dream-song healing (Malaysia): Roseman M. 1991 'Healing Sounds from the Malaysian Rainforest' — paired bamboo-tube stampers (two pitches, duple pulse), women's chorus, trance; a textbook trance-induction soundscape.
- Mazatec velada (María Sabina): Wasson, Cowan & Rhodes 1974 'María Sabina and her Mazatec Mushroom Velada' (with recordings) — chant, clapping and syllabic rhythm in a psilocybin ceremony; Huichol/Wixárika peyote pilgrimage songs (Furst, Myerhoff 1974) — both expected Mesoamerican items.
- Warao shamanic curing songs: Olsen D.A. 1996 'Music of the Warao of Venezuela' — documents upward microtonal pitch drift during curing as a marker of power, and the hebu mataro rattle (~ pulse data in transcriptions).
- Tukano/Desana yajé: Reichel-Dolmatoff G. 1971 'Amazonian Cosmos' and 1975 'The Shaman and the Jaguar' — phosphene/geometric-vision analysis linked to chant and rattle, foundational for the 'visual music' idea credited to the Shipibo.
- European trance-music traditions: Apulian tarantism (De Martino 1961 'La terra del rimorso'; pizzica/tarantella ~140-170 bpm) and Greek Anastenaria fire-walking (Danforth 1989; lyra and daouli drum) — the only well-documented European analogues to the possession material cited.
- Sri Lankan tovil/Sanni Yakuma exorcism (Kapferer 1983 'A Celebration of Demons') and Kerala theyyam — drum-driven South Asian possession rituals with detailed rhythmic analyses.
- Japanese itako (Osorezan) blind mediums with azusa-yumi bow and rosary, and Okinawan yuta; Taiwanese tâng-ki spirit mediums — East Asian spirit-medium sound practices absent from the lens.
- Densmore's metronome tables (voice vs drum tempi, often independent) across Chippewa, Teton Sioux, Papago, Yuman-Yaqui monographs — the largest early quantitative tempo dataset for Native North American ceremonial music, contradicting several 'no published bpm' notes.
- Siberian primary recordings with measurable drum tempi: Nganasan shaman Tubyaku Kosterkin (Hoppál/Lintrop films; Musicaphon 'Siberian Arctic' recordings) and Corine Sombrun's Mongolian trance EEG (Flor-Henry, Shapiro & Sombrun 2017, Cogent Psychology 4:1313522) — cited only in passing.
- Explicit statement that Solfeggio frequencies (396-963 Hz, incl. 528 Hz), 432 Hz 'natural tuning' and chakra-frequency tables appear in no indigenous tradition surveyed (the one 2026 source that uses 528 Hz is a modern Māori-led ecology project, not an ancestral practice).

## 4. Modern esoteric and consciousness-technology systems

*Lens scope:* MODERN ESOTERIC, NEW-AGE AND CONSCIOUSNESS-TECHNOLOGY SYSTEMS (Monroe/Hemi-Sync, CIA Gateway, SRI remote viewing, Solfeggio, Cousto, 432 Hz, Tesla/Rife lineages, sound-healing practitioners, Awakened Mind/Holosync/AVS devices, manifestation systems, preset lore). Verified by web search/fetch where noted; search budget was exhausted near the end, so a handful of items (flagged 'from memory; verify') rest on prior knowledge.

### Monroe / Hemi-Sync — the 1975 patent US 3,884,218

- **Evidence level:** moderate
- **Finding:** Robert Monroe's first patent, 'Method of inducing and maintaining various stages of sleep in the human being', was filed 30 Sep 1970 and granted 20 May 1975 (Monroe Industries). It is NOT a binaural-beat patent: claim 1 is amplitude-modulating a pleasing repetitive audio signal (example: a 'steady 500 cycle hum') with an EEG-like waveform. The description lists bands (alertness 10–40 cps; alpha 8–13; stage-1 theta 4–8; spindles ~14; delta ~1 cps) but gives no carrier/beat schedule. The Hemi-Sync name/trademark and the 1958 OBE onset are Monroe's own account; R&D into sound and sleep started at RAM Enterprises in 1956; The Monroe Institute became an independent nonprofit in 1985 (Faber, VA).
- **Usable parameters:** Monaural/AM alternative to binaural: amplitude-modulate a ~500 Hz hum (or any pleasant drone) with an envelope at 8–13 Hz (relaxed), 4–8 Hz (stage-1/hypnagogic), ~1 Hz (deep). Patent claim 3 adds an auto-off timer.
- **Notes:** Evidence level refers to the historical facts; efficacy claims are separate (see Hemi-Sync research finding). Online claims that this patent lists '10 Hz down to 1.5 Hz' are not in the patent text.
- **Source:** Google Patents US3884218A https://patents.google.com/patent/US3884218A/en ; Wikipedia 'Hemi-Sync' and 'Monroe Institute'; Psi Encyclopedia 'Robert Monroe' https://psi-encyclopedia.spr.ac.uk/articles/robert-monroe/

### Monroe / Hemi-Sync — the 1993–94 patents with actual numbers (US 5,213,562; US 5,356,368)

- **Evidence level:** moderate
- **Finding:** Monroe's later patents give the concrete recipe. 1993 patent: example 100 Hz left / 104 Hz right = 4 Hz binaural beat; 'the most effective harmonic carrier would be 275 Hz'; 'as many as 100 separate carrier pairs may be used'; 'phased pink sound' mixed at least 10 dB below the beat signals; figure captions show stacked multi-beat sets (1.5+4+6 Hz; 2+4+7 Hz; 0.5+3+4 Hz; 1.5+2+4 Hz). 1994 patent 'septon' example: L 200/204/208 Hz vs R 204/208/212 Hz, producing simultaneous 4 Hz binaural AND 4 Hz monaural beats; wake-up uses 400/416 Hz (16 Hz beta); pink-noise phase sweep period 6 s, comb-filter sweep ~1/8 Hz; sleep program segmented 0–5, 5–20, 20–40, 40–65, 65–80, 80–90 min; voice inserts every 40 s in the first segment.
- **Usable parameters:** Carrier stack: L {200,204,208} / R {204,208,212} Hz (or any base 100–300 Hz with 4 Hz spacing); multi-beat layering e.g. 1.5 + 4 + 6 Hz simultaneously at decreasing amplitude; pink noise ≥10 dB under beats, slowly phase-modulated (6 s cycle); segment boundaries at 5/20/40/65/80/90 min; beta wake-up 16 Hz on 400/416 Hz.
- **Notes:** The patents never name Focus 10/12/15/21 or give per-Focus frequencies; those remain unpublished/proprietary.
- **Source:** US5213562A https://patents.google.com/patent/US5213562A/en ; US5356368A https://patents.google.com/patent/US5356368A/en

### Monroe Focus levels — definitions and the (unpublished) frequency sets

- **Evidence level:** traditional (a lineage's own teaching, not a tested claim)
- **Finding:** Focus levels are a lineage-defined map, not measured states: C-1 ordinary waking; Focus 10 'mind awake, body asleep'; 12 'expanded awareness' (tools: problem solving, patterning, color breathing, energy bar tool, remote viewing, living body map); 15 'no time' (CIA memo: travel into the PAST, time as 'a huge wheel with spokes'; <5% of trainees reach it in a program); 21 'bridge/edge of time-space, other energy systems' (memo: FUTURE); 22 impaired human consciousness; 23 recently deceased/stuck; 24–26 belief-system territories; 27 'the Park'/reception center (Lifeline program, from 'Far Journeys' 1985); 34/35 'the Gathering'; 42 'I-There clusters'; 49 beyond ('Ultimate Journey' 1994). TMI has never published the beat frequencies; all numbers online are reverse-engineered guesses. The best-documented third-party reconstruction (SINE, explicitly 'not official') uses: F10 descend 10→7 Hz over 15–20 min; F12 4–5 Hz (original tapes shift ~7→4 Hz over ~10 min); F15 2–3 Hz; F21 0.5–2 Hz; carriers 200 Hz; descent rate ~0.5 Hz/min. The Monroe UK/Wikipedia example: 170 Hz vs 174 Hz = 4 Hz.
- **Usable parameters:** Ladder: F10 = 10→7 Hz (15–20 min); F12 = 4–5 Hz (hold 10–15 min); F15 = 2–3 Hz; F21 = 0.5–2 Hz; carriers 170–200 Hz; ramp ≤0.5 Hz/min; name segments by Focus number in the guide.
- **Notes:** 'Traditional' = defined within the Monroe lineage; no independent evidence that the Focus numbers correspond to distinct EEG states or to 'other energy systems'.
- **Source:** CIA memo (Wikisource) https://en.wikisource.org/wiki/Analysis_and_Assessment_of_Gateway_Process ; Monroe 'Far Journeys' (1985), 'Ultimate Journey' (1994); SINE reconstruction https://sine-immersive.com/blog/gateway-tapes-hemi-sync-guide ; Wikipedia Hemi-Sync

### CIA 'Analysis and Assessment of Gateway Process' (LTC Wayne M. McDonnell, 9 June 1983)

- **Evidence level:** none (no evidence)
- **Finding:** A US Army INSCOM staff assessment of whether to send personnel to Monroe's Gateway, declassified 2003 (CIA Reading Room) with page 25 missing; page 25 was released by The Monroe Institute (Jenny Whedbee) to Vice's Thobey Campion, published 8 Apr 2021 — it covers 'the Absolute' as 'the ceiling', time as energy in motion, the universe as hologram/'cosmic egg', spirals in religions, and 'Know Thyself'. The memo is a literature synthesis (Bentov 'Stalking the Wild Pendulum', Bohm implicate order, Pribram holographic brain, Melissa Jager on Hemi-Sync); it ran no experiments. Specific numbers in it: body resonance ~7 Hz; brain 'transfers' at 6.8–7.5 Hz into the ionospheric cavity resonating 7–7.5 Hz; kundalini vibration 4–7 Hz; normal beta 30–40 cps; one OBE tape includes a beta signal 'around 2877.3 CPS'; 'click-out' below Planck's distance 10^-33 cm. It asserts Focus 15 = past, Focus 21 = future, and that consciousness can access past/present/future because they coexist in the universal hologram — stated as postulates. Conclusion: 'There is a sound, rational basis in terms of physical science parameters'; recommends phased training A–K ending with Focus 15/21 work from the OBE state and a 3-person 'multi-focus' method.
- **Usable parameters:** A 'transfer window' hold at 7.0–7.5 Hz (memo's ionosphere-matching band) before descending; a very faint high tone near 2877 Hz as an homage layer (≤ -40 dB) during the 'out-of-body' segment; label segments 'Focus 15 — no time' and 'Focus 21 — the edge'.
- **Notes:** Evidence level 'none' refers to the time-travel/escape-space-time claims; the document itself is authentic (strong as an artifact). Its physics (Planck-scale 'click-out', 7 Hz ionosphere coupling) is speculative and not accepted.
- **Source:** Wikisource full text; CIA Reading Room CIA-RDP96-00788R001700210016-5; Vice 'Found: Page 25' https://www.vice.com/en/article/found-page-25-of-the-cias-gateway-report-on-astral-projection/ ; Black Vault; archive.org 1983-analysis-and-assessment-of-gateway-process

### Gateway Experience tape structure (preparatory process)

- **Evidence level:** traditional (a lineage's own teaching, not a tested claim)
- **Finding:** Every Gateway exercise opens with a fixed preparatory sequence: (1) Energy Conversion Box — visualize placing worries in a box; (2) Resonant Tuning — hum/ tone on the exhale with a choir drone on the tape 'to create vibration, especially in the head' (memo wording), with pulsed breathing; (3) Affirmation — 'I am more than my physical body… I deeply desire to expand… to experience…'; (4) Resonant Energy Balloon (REBAL) — energy from crown down to feet and back up forming a protective shell ('reminiscent of the cosmic egg'); (5) count 1→10 into Focus 10; later exercises add 'Release and Recharge', 'Exploration Sleep', 'Free Flow'. Home-study 'Gateway Experience' is sold as Waves (I Discovery: Orientation, Intro Focus 10, Advanced F10, Release & Recharge, Exploration Sleep, Free Flow 10; II Threshold: Intro F12, Problem Solving, One-Month Patterning, Color Breathing, Energy Bar Tool, Living Body Map; III Freedom: Lift-Off, Remote Viewing, Vectors, Five Questions, Energy Food, First-Stage Separation; IV Adventure incl. Compoint 12, NVC I/II, Null Point, Intro F15; later Waves V–VIII add F21 and beyond). Wave titles/exercises are from TMI catalog descriptions (from memory; verify against current TMI listing).
- **Usable parameters:** Preamble 4–6 min: 60 s box visualization, 2–3 min hum-on-exhale with a sustained choir/drone (e.g., 136.1 Hz or C 256 Hz), 45 s affirmation, 60 s REBAL crown→feet→crown visualization (pair with a 5–6 s head-to-toe spatial sweep), then count 1–10 as the beat ramps 10→7 Hz.
- **Notes:** McDonnell calls REBAL the 'energy balloon'. Exercise names beyond Wave I–III are less certain.
- **Source:** CIA memo components section (Wikisource); TMI/Hemi-Sync catalog (verify); Monroe 'Journeys Out of the Body' (1971)

### Monroe's 'vibrational state' and roll-out technique (Journeys Out of the Body, 1971)

- **Evidence level:** anecdotal
- **Finding:** Monroe describes OBEs as preceded by 'vibrations' — a surging, roaring, electric-like sensation; a 'ring of sparks' sweeping head→toes→head with a cycle of about 5 seconds; a 'Condition D' (deep relaxation, mind awake/body asleep, best reached after sleep). His method: relax, enter hypnagogia, deepen until body awareness fades, induce vibrations (visualize them ~1 foot above head, pull into body), 'smooth' and speed them up until they become a steady hum-like state, then practice partial separation (a hand), then full separation by 'lift-out' (float up) or 'roll-out' (mentally, not physically, turn over as in bed).
- **Usable parameters:** 'Vibration' section (minutes ~20–35): add a slow 0.2 Hz (5-s period) front-to-back/head-to-feet spatial sweep of the drone, a gradual increase of beat amplitude modulation depth, and a narration cue to 'smooth and speed the vibrations'; roll-out cue at the end of the deepest segment.
- **Notes:** Identical sensations (vibration, buzzing, paralysis, floating) are well-documented features of hypnagogia/sleep paralysis; no evidence of literal exteriorization.
- **Source:** Monroe, Journeys Out of the Body (Doubleday 1971), archive.org scan https://ia800703.us.archive.org/28/items/journeys_out_body/journeys_out_body.pdf ; sacred-texts BoS 186 Monroe techniques summary

### Hemi-Sync research record and a major caveat

- **Evidence level:** weak
- **Finding:** Published trials: Kliempt et al. 1999 (double-blind, Hemi-Sync reduced intra-operative fentanyl need), Lewis et al. 2004 (replication positive), Dabu-Bondoc et al. 2003 (no propofol reduction). A 2002 University of Virginia presentation (Stone et al., Society for Psychophysiological Research) found the EEG changes disappeared when Monroe's standard headphones were replaced with air-conduction headphones, raising the possibility of an electromagnetic (transducer) artifact rather than an acoustic entrainment effect. TMI's own Mind Mirror study of SAM meditations reports no results.
- **Usable parameters:** None beyond: use ordinary headphones; expect at most modest relaxation/analgesic-adjunct effects.
- **Notes:** Evidence for 'altered states' is anecdotal; evidence for small anxiolytic/analgesic effects is mixed-weak.
- **Source:** Wikipedia 'Hemi-Sync' (cites Kliempt 1999 Anaesthesia; Lewis 2004; Dabu-Bondoc 2003 Anesth Analg; Stone 2002 UVA)

### SRI remote viewing / Stargate and its relation to Monroe

- **Evidence level:** weak
- **Finding:** Remote viewing research began at SRI in 1972 (Puthoff, Targ; initial CIA funding $49,909); Ingo Swann proposed 'coordinate remote viewing' (targets given only as geographic coordinates), later formalized as the 6-stage CRV protocol (1980s). Joseph McMoneagle was 'Remote Viewer No. 1' from 1978, retired 1984, consulted to 1993, and was sent to the Monroe Institute during training; the Army evaluated TMI in 1978 and sent officers in 1983 (the McDonnell memo was part of that). CRV itself has no sound protocol; it is a paper-based structured protocol preceded by a relaxation 'cool-down'. The 1995 AIR review (Utts/Hyman) ended Stargate, finding no operational intelligence value despite statistically anomalous lab results (from memory; verify).
- **Usable parameters:** Only the structure: a 10–15 min alpha (~10 Hz) 'cool-down' before any 'viewing'/intention segment; Gateway Wave III contains a 'Remote Viewing' exercise at Focus 12 (4–5 Hz).
- **Notes:** Remote viewing is classed as parapsychology; no accepted evidence.
- **Source:** Wikipedia 'Parapsychology research at SRI', 'Joseph McMoneagle', 'Russell Targ', 'Monroe Institute'; FAS Stargate page https://irp.fas.org/program/collect/stargate.htm

### Solfeggio frequencies — real origin

- **Evidence level:** none (no evidence)
- **Finding:** The nine 'ancient Solfeggio frequencies' date to 1999: Joseph Puleo (naturopath) claimed a vision c. 1974 leading him to apply 'Pythagorean reduction' (digit-summing) to verse numbers in Numbers 7:12–83, producing six repeating numbers (396, 417, 528, 639, 741, 852); Leonard Horowitz co-authored 'Healing Codes for the Biological Apocalypse' (1999) and added 174, 285, 963 by extending the pattern (each adjacent pair differs by 111 Hz; every tone's digits reduce to 3, 6 or 9). The name borrows from Guido d'Arezzo's (c. 1025) solmization syllables from the hymn 'Ut queant laxis' (hymn and syllables are real; the hymn's melody is not tuned to these Hz values and no medieval source mentions them). Assigned meanings (Horowitz/Puleo and later marketers): 174 pain/security; 285 tissue; 396 liberating guilt/fear; 417 undoing situations/facilitating change; 528 'transformation and miracles', 'DNA repair', 'love frequency'; 639 relationships; 741 awakening intuition/expression; 852 'returning to spiritual order'/third eye; 963 'oneness', 'God frequency', pineal/crown. Extended '111/222/333/444' sets are 2010s YouTube 'angel number' additions with no author of record.
- **Usable parameters:** If used: 396/417/528/639/741/852/963 Hz as pure or lightly-detuned drones/overtone shimmer; the 111 Hz spacing means successive tones are inharmonic with each other — use one at a time or as a slow ascending 'ladder' (e.g., 7 tones × 1–2 min). 963 Hz is too high to carry a clean binaural beat (perception degrades above ~1 kHz carrier) — use it as an overtone layer, not the beat carrier.
- **Notes:** Small studies on 528 Hz (e.g., Akimoto et al. 2018, cortisol/oxytocin, n≈9; from memory, verify) are tiny and unreplicated — weak at best. Horowitz also popularized the 'Rockefeller 440 Hz' conspiracy.
- **Source:** Horowitz & Puleo, Healing Codes for the Biological Apocalypse (Tetrahedron, 1999); Sound Medicine Academy critique https://www.soundmedicineacademy.com/pages/sound-healing-blog/solfeggio-frequencies ; omlumi review; miraclefrequencies.org origins

### 432 Hz vs 440 Hz — history and evidence

- **Evidence level:** weak
- **Finding:** Concert pitch varied widely historically (~392–450+ Hz). France set A=435 in 1859; a 1939 London conference recommended A=440, adopted as ISO 16 in 1975. Verdi argued (1884 letter; Boito at the 1881 Milan congress) for a lower 'scientific' pitch around 432/435. The modern 432 cult was launched by Lyndon LaRouche's Schiller Institute 'Verdi tuning' campaign (1988–89), which also invented the claim that Goebbels imposed 440 Hz — fact-checked as false; the Rockefeller version was added by Horowitz. Maria Renold (anthroposophy; 'Intervals, Scales, Tones and the Concert Pitch C=128 Hz', German 1985) advocated C=128/A=432 and a 'scale of twelve fifths' after Steiner's remark. '432 is Pythagorean' only in the sense that 432 = 27/16 × 256. Physiological evidence: Calamassi & Pomponi 2019 (n=33 crossover; small heart-rate decrease with 432 vs 440) — weak, unreplicated; Emoto's 432-Hz water crystals — no controlled evidence (Radin/Emoto 2008 JSE is the only quasi-controlled attempt; widely criticized).
- **Usable parameters:** Tune musical layers to A=432 (C=256, G=384, F=341.33, E=320, D=288, B=480) if desired — it is harmless and pairs neatly with the C=256 'scientific pitch' systems (Goldman, Beaulieu, Hero). 432/440 has no bearing on beat frequencies.
- **Notes:** 'Weak' refers to the single small physiological study; the 'universal/healing frequency' and Nazi claims are false.
- **Source:** Lead Stories fact check https://leadstories.com/hoax-alert/2022/10/fact-check-tuning-standard-of-a-440-hz-was-not-driven-by-nazi-and-rockefeller-desire-for-discord.html ; Wikipedia 'Concert pitch', 'Masaru Emoto'; J. Popular Music Studies 33(1) 'Perfect Pitch: 432 Hz Music…' https://online.ucpress.edu/jpms/article/33/1/137/116319

### Tesla — quotes, 3-6-9, and the 11.78 Hz 'Earth resonance'

- **Evidence level:** none (no evidence)
- **Finding:** 'If you want to find the secrets of the universe, think in terms of energy, frequency and vibration' appears in none of Tesla's writings, patents or major biographies (Seifer, Carlson); its only provenance is a second-hand recollection attributed to Ralph Bergstresser, and it spread via 1980s–2000s New Age literature. '3, 6, 9 are the key to the universe' has no primary source; fact-checkers trace it to 2009+ blogs and the 2020–21 TikTok '369 method'. Tesla's real Earth-resonance work: US patent 787,412 (filed 16 May 1900, granted 18 Apr 1905) estimates the round-trip time of a wave to the antipode as 'not less than one-twelfth or probably 0.08484 of a second' (≈11.79 Hz) and says the lowest usable frequency 'would appear to be six per second' — a calculation, not a measurement, and distinct from the Schumann resonance (predicted by W.O. Schumann 1952; first clearly measured by Balser & Wagner 1960 at ~7.83 Hz).
- **Usable parameters:** If a Tesla nod is wanted: 11.79 Hz (=1/0.08484 s) low-alpha beat, or 6 Hz theta (his 'lowest frequency'); 3/6/9 Hz beats are legitimate delta/theta/alpha values regardless of the myth.
- **Notes:** Evidence 'none' refers to the quotes and numerology; the patent text is verified.
- **Source:** Truth or Fiction '369 theory' https://www.truthorfiction.com/nikola-tesla-369-theory/ ; Metaphysical Hub misattribution essay; Google Patents US787412A https://patents.google.com/patent/US787412A/en ; Wikipedia 'Schumann resonances'

### Schumann resonance 7.83 Hz and harmonics

- **Evidence level:** moderate
- **Finding:** The Earth-ionosphere cavity resonates at ~7.83 Hz fundamental with harmonics ~14.1–14.3, 20.3–20.8, 26.3–27.3, 32.5–33.8 Hz (values differ by measurement and source; they drift ±~0.5 Hz diurnally/seasonally). Claims that listening to 7.83 Hz 'grounds' or 'synchronizes' people with Earth are unsupported; the memo's '7–7.5 Hz transfer' is a 1983 speculation (Bentov). McKusick's Sonic Slider fork is 93.96 Hz = 12 × 7.83.
- **Usable parameters:** 7.83 Hz beat (theta/alpha border) as a 'landing pad' hold; harmonics 14.1 and 20.3 Hz as light beta layers for the return; 93.96 Hz as a low drone/pulse.
- **Notes:** Physics is strong; biological/spiritual claims are none.
- **Source:** Wikipedia 'Schumann resonances', 'Winfried Otto Schumann'; Biofield Tuning blog https://www.biofieldtuning.com/post/the-tuning-forks-used-in-biofield-tuning

### Rife, Lakhovsky, Beck, Clark — electromedicine lineage

- **Evidence level:** none (no evidence)
- **Finding:** Royal Rife (1930s) claimed each pathogen has a 'mortal oscillatory rate' (MOR) destroyed by a radio-frequency 'Rife Ray'; modern 'Rife machines' (from John Crane's 1950s versions) output audio-range square waves (e.g., 727, 787, 880, 2008, 5000, 10000 Hz lists) through electrodes; reviews find no evidence and devices have been subject to enforcement. Georges Lakhovsky (The Secret of Life, 1925; Multiple Wave Oscillator patented 1931/US 1,962,565) claimed cells are oscillators and his MWO radiated ~750 kHz–3 GHz; unproven. Bob Beck's 1990s 'Beck Protocol' (microcurrent blood electrification at ~3.92 Hz/50–100 µA, magnetic pulser, colloidal silver, ozonated water) — no evidence. Hulda Clark's 'Zapper' (The Cure for All Diseases, 1995; ~30 kHz, 9 V, 7-min cycles) — FTC 2004 stipulated judgment/refunds; FDA called her devices fraudulent; pacemaker arrhythmia case reported.
- **Usable parameters:** None transferable to audio. If a 'Rife' number is quoted in marketing, note it refers to electrode/RF devices, not sound.
- **Notes:** Beck 3.92 Hz figure from memory; verify.
- **Source:** Wikipedia 'Royal Rife', 'Georges Lakhovsky', 'Hulda Regehr Clark'; Healthline Rife review; Oncothermia Journal 2023 'Cancer-specific resonances' (critical)

### Jeffrey Thompson — epsilon and lambda

- **Evidence level:** anecdotal
- **Finding:** Thompson (Center for Neuroacoustic Research, Carlsbad CA, founded 1988) coined 'epsilon' for sub-delta EEG below 0.5 Hz (down to ~1 cycle per 40 s) and 'lambda'/'hyper-gamma' for ~100–200 Hz, claiming both accompany 'ecstatic states', OBE and 'spiritual' experiences in expert meditators, with epsilon acting as a carrier wave for lambda. Source is his own article and vendor copy (iAwake, SHRF); no peer-reviewed validation. Mainstream EEG above ~80 Hz is dominated by muscle artifact; genuine infraslow (<0.1 Hz) oscillations exist but are studied with DC-EEG/fMRI, not entrainment.
- **Usable parameters:** Epsilon segment: beat 0.1–0.5 Hz (e.g., 0.25 Hz = one cycle/4 s) for 5–8 min inside the deepest part; 'lambda' 100–200 Hz cannot be a binaural beat (limit ~30–35 Hz) — implement as isochronic/AM pulse at low depth or as a 100–200 Hz drone.
- **Notes:** Founded-1988 date from memory; verify.
- **Source:** Thompson, 'Epsilon, Gamma, HyperGamma, Lambda Brainwave Activity and Ecstatic States of Consciousness' (SHRF PDF) https://soundhealingresearchfoundation.org/wp-content/uploads/2021/12/Epsilon-Gamma-HyperGamma-Lambda-Brainwave-Activity-and-Ecstatic-States-of-Consciousness.pdf ; iAwake 'Epsilon' page

### Tom Kenyon — Acoustic Brain Research and the Hathors

- **Evidence level:** anecdotal
- **Finding:** Kenyon founded Acoustic Brain Research (ABR) in 1983 to study psychoacoustics; his recordings use 'BioPulse Technology' (tones associated with brain states mixed into the music) and multi-octave extended vocal toning; since 1987 he has channeled 'the Hathors' (described as inter-dimensional beings from an 'ascended intergalactic civilization'); 'Sound Transformations' is a collection of live tone-healing sessions; 'The Cave of Altruin' uses a 'rotating acoustic vortex'. No published frequencies or independent studies.
- **Usable parameters:** Techniques (not numbers): layered vocal toning across 3–4 octaves; slowly rotating ('vortex') panning of the vocal layer (0.05–0.2 Hz rotation); invite listener to attend to the sounds themselves rather than visualize.
- **Notes:** 1983 date is Kenyon's own biography.
- **Source:** tomkenyon.com/about; tomkenyon.com/listening; tomkenyon.com/the-cave-of-altruin-hathors-2

### Jonathan Goldman — 'Frequency + Intent = Healing', vowel-chakra system, C=256 scale

- **Evidence level:** traditional (a lineage's own teaching, not a tested claim)
- **Finding:** Goldman (Healing Sounds, 1992; Sound Healers Association 1982; 'Chakra Frequencies/Tantra of Sound' 2005; 'Sacred Gateways' chants) teaches the formula 'Frequency + Intent = Healing' and a seven-vowel chakra ladder: UH (root) – OOO (sacral) – OH (navel/solar plexus) – AH (heart) – EYE (throat) – AYE (third eye) – EEE (crown), sung from lowest to highest pitch; his Chakra Tuner app assigns just-intonation notes on C=256: C 256 (root), D 288, E 320, F 341.3 (heart, AH), G 384, A 426.7, B 480 (crown). Pitch-to-body mapping (low = lower trunk, high = head) is stated to vary per person.
- **Usable parameters:** Optional 7-stage toning section: 256/288/320/341.3/384/426.7/480 Hz drones, 60–90 s each with vowel cue UH→EEE; or map the beat ladder to it (root=delta … crown=gamma).
- **Notes:** Vowel assignments vary across Goldman's books and copycat sites; the set above matches his own excerpts for UH/OH/AH/EYE.
- **Source:** healingsounds.com booklets (DN1111 and 11:11 Divine Name seminar PDFs); ask.video Goldman interview; Chakra Frequencies (Goldman & Goldman, 2005)

### Maman, Berendt, Gaynor, Hero — sound-healing literature

- **Evidence level:** anecdotal
- **Finding:** Fabien Maman (with biologist Hélène Grimal, Univ. Jussieu Paris, 1981) photographed HeLa cells exposed to instruments/voice ascending through a scale, claiming cells 'exploded' after ~14–21 min, notably with the voice — reported only in his own book 'The Role of Music in the Twenty-First Century' (1997), never replicated or peer-reviewed. Joachim-Ernst Berendt's 'Nada Brahma: Die Welt ist Klang' (1983; English 'The World Is Sound' 1987) popularized Cousto and the 'Om = 136.1 Hz' lore. Oncologist Mitchell Gaynor ('Sounds of Healing', 1999) used crystal singing bowls and chant as adjunct care at Strang Cancer Prevention Center (clinical anecdote). Barbara Hero's Lambdoma (1990s) is a 16×16 matrix of whole-number ratios (rows harmonics, columns subharmonics) on a 1:1 fundamental of 256 Hz, mapped to organs/chakras by assertion.
- **Usable parameters:** Just-intonation harmonic field on 256 Hz: ratios 1/1, 9/8, 5/4, 4/3, 3/2, 5/3, 15/8, 2/1 (256, 288, 320, 341.3, 384, 426.7, 480, 512 Hz) and subharmonics 128, 85.3, 64 Hz for a Lambdoma-style drone bed.
- **Notes:** Maman's cell claims are often cited as 'proof' of sound destroying cancer — they are not.
- **Source:** Hero & Foulkrod 1999 'The Lambdoma matrix and harmonic intervals' https://www.lambdoma.com/pdfs/the-lambdoma-matrix-and-harmonic-intervals.pdf ; Maman 1997; Berendt 1983/1987; Gaynor 1999

### Eileen McKusick (Biofield Tuning) and John Beaulieu (BioSonics) tuning forks

- **Evidence level:** none (no evidence)
- **Finding:** McKusick ('Tuning the Human Biofield', 2014) works 'in the biofield' (claimed 5–6 ft field with a chronological timeline) using unweighted forks 174 Hz (field 'combing'), 417 and 528 Hz (Solfeggio), plus 144 and 222 Hz in some kits, and the weighted Sonic Slider at 93.96 Hz (= 12 × 7.83 Schumann). Beaulieu ('Music and Sound in the Healing Arts', 1987; BioSonics) sells 'Body Tuners' C 256 Hz and G 384 Hz (a perfect fifth, 3:2) and weighted 'Otto' forks at 32, 64 and 128 Hz applied to bone; Beaulieu co-authored a 2003 hypothesis paper (Salamon et al., Med Sci Monit) proposing nitric-oxide release from sound relaxation — unreplicated. Neither system has controlled outcome evidence.
- **Usable parameters:** Drone options: 256 + 384 Hz fifth; 128 Hz 'Otto' low pulse (can be AM'd at 4–8 Hz); 93.96 Hz sub-drone; fork-like decaying envelopes (strike every 10–20 s, ~8 s decay).
- **Notes:** 'Otto 128' is Beaulieu's product, not McKusick's.
- **Source:** biofieldtuning.com blog on forks; biofieldtuningstore.com Sonic Slider page; Beaulieu 1987 (from memory; verify Otto/Body Tuner specs on biosonics.com)

### Halpern, Iasos, Laraaji — New Age ambient origins

- **Evidence level:** anecdotal
- **Finding:** Steven Halpern's 'Spectrum Suite' (1975; reissued 1976) is one of the first New Age albums: static, pulse-free electric-piano pieces, each in a key intended to 'resonate' a chakra (ascending), marketed via health-food stores ('anti-frantic' music). Iasos's 'Inter-Dimensional Music' (1975) is 'paradise music' he reports hearing internally under guidance from 'Vista'; in 1989 Joel Funk (Plymouth State) found near-death experiencers rated Iasos's 'The Angels of Comfort' (1978) closest to music heard in NDEs. Laraaji's 'Day of Radiance' (1980, Brian Eno, Ambient 3) uses an electrified zither; he teaches laughter meditation.
- **Usable parameters:** Aesthetic template: no tempo/rhythm, slow (30–90 s) harmonic drift, long reverb tails, shimmering upper partials (zither/bells), ascending key centers across the piece (e.g., C→B over 7 sections).
- **Notes:** Funk's study is a small survey, not evidence of 'other dimensions'.
- **Source:** Wikipedia 'Steven Halpern', 'Iasos (musician)', 'Laraaji'

### C. Maxwell Cade & Anna Wise — the Awakened Mind EEG pattern

- **Evidence level:** moderate
- **Finding:** Cade and engineer Geoffrey Blundell built the Mind Mirror (1976), a band-amplitude EEG display; Cade recorded >4,000 people incl. yogis/Zen masters and published 'The Awakened Mind' (with Nona Coxhead, 1979). The 'fifth state'/Awakened Mind pattern = simultaneous presence of quiet beta (14–30 Hz), strong alpha (8–14), theta (4–8) and delta (0.5–4) — a 'symphony' rather than one dominant band; ordinary consciousness shows beta+delta without the alpha/theta bridge; the meditation patterns (categories 2–4) suppress beta while the Awakened Mind retains it. Later 'Evolved Mind' = whole-brain gamma synchrony (30–100 Hz). Anna Wise brought the Mind Mirror to the US in 1981 ('The High-Performance Mind', 1995; practitioner training 2001).
- **Usable parameters:** 'Awakened-mind stack' for the integration/return phase: simultaneous beats at ~1.5 Hz (delta), 5.5 Hz (theta), 10 Hz (alpha) and 15 Hz (low beta) with amplitudes decreasing upward (e.g., 0 / -3 / -6 / -12 dB), mirroring Monroe's multi-beat patents.
- **Notes:** Pattern description is well-documented; its 'spiritual' interpretation is not.
- **Source:** Institute for the Awakened Mind https://institutefortheawakenedmind.com/awakened-mind/ ; Mind Mirror Portal biographies; Cade & Coxhead 1979

### Les Fehmi — Open Focus (from memory; search budget exhausted)

- **Evidence level:** weak
- **Finding:** Fehmi (Princeton Biofeedback Centre, 1970s; 'The Open-Focus Brain', 2007 with Jim Robbins) found that imagining space/distance/absence ('Can you imagine the space between your eyes?', 'the space the room occupies') rapidly produced high-amplitude, phase-synchronous alpha across five EEG channels, whereas effortful focusing did not; he treats 'objectless imagery' as the key to synchronous alpha and diffuse attention. Evidence is case series and clinical reports.
- **Usable parameters:** Narration script for the alpha (10 Hz) segment: 10–15 'space' questions, 15–20 s apart ('imagine the space between your ears… the space your body occupies… the distance between you and the ceiling').
- **Notes:** Not verified in this run (Wikipedia page 404).
- **Source:** Fehmi & Robbins, The Open-Focus Brain (Trumpeter, 2007); Fehmi's Princeton Biofeedback writings (verify)

### Holosync (Centerpointe, Bill Harris)

- **Evidence level:** weak
- **Finding:** Harris began experimenting in 1985 and launched Holosync publicly in 1989; entry program 'Awakening Prologue' has two tracks: 'Dive' (ramps from alpha down to delta) and 'Immersion' (holds delta); each successive level is 'stronger' because the carrier frequency is lowered, over a 12+ level, multi-year program; marketing cites Oster 1973 and claims EEG-verifiable effects, but no independent EEG study exists. Harris died 2018.
- **Usable parameters:** Two-stage architecture: ~30 min ramp alpha→delta (e.g., 10→1 Hz), then ~30 min delta hold; progressive 'deepening' expressed by lowering carriers (e.g., 300 → 200 → 150 → 100 Hz across sessions/versions) at constant beat.
- **Notes:** Exact carrier values are proprietary and undisclosed.
- **Source:** centerpointe.com 'What Is Holosync'; shop.centerpointe.com Awakening Prologue; user reviews (howtolucid, transcendzen)

### Gerald Oster 1973 and the psychoacoustic limits of binaural beats

- **Evidence level:** **strong**
- **Finding:** Heinrich Wilhelm Dove described binaural beats in 1839; Gerald Oster's 'Auditory Beats in the Brain' (Scientific American, Oct 1973) consolidated the field. Beats are perceived only for carriers below roughly 1000 Hz (clearest ~200–500 Hz) and frequency differences below ~30–35 Hz; the percept arises in the superior olivary complex/inferior colliculus. Monroe's patent recommends ~275 Hz as the most effective carrier.
- **Usable parameters:** Carriers 100–400 Hz (sweet spot ~200–300 Hz); beat ≤ 30 Hz; L/R level-matched; pure or lightly harmonic tones; for 'gamma' ≥ 30 Hz use isochronic/AM pulses, not binaural.
- **Notes:** These constraints are the only 'strong' science in this lens.
- **Source:** Oster 1973 Sci Am 229(4):94–102; Wikipedia 'Binaural beats'; hearinghealthmatters 'Binaural Beat Therapy Revisited'

### Does binaural-beat entrainment actually happen? (the evidence base)

- **Evidence level:** weak
- **Finding:** Ingendoh, Posny & Heine 2023 (PLOS ONE, PRISMA review of 14 EEG studies in healthy adults): 5 supported the entrainment hypothesis, 8 contradicted it, 1 mixed — 'inconclusive', methods not comparable. Garcia-Argibay, Santed & Reales 2019 (Psychological Research meta-analysis, 22 studies/35 effect sizes): small-to-medium overall benefit on anxiety, memory, attention and pain, larger with longer exposure; 40 Hz beats showed the strongest cognitive effect. Jirakittayakorn & Wongsawat 2017 (Frontiers): 6 Hz beat on 250 Hz carrier for 30 min increased theta/frontal-midline theta. A 2020 Sci Rep attentional-blink study found 40 Hz (not 16 Hz) beats improved training and MEG showed gamma entrainment. Sustained-attention reviews are negative.
- **Usable parameters:** Exposure ≥ 20–30 min per state; 6 Hz on 250 Hz is the best-documented theta recipe; 40 Hz for alertness/cognition.
- **Notes:** 'Weak' for EEG entrainment; 'moderate' at most for subjective relaxation/anxiety effects.
- **Source:** Ingendoh et al. 2023 https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article?id=10.1371/journal.pone.0286023 ; Garcia-Argibay et al. 2019 Psychol Res 73:357–372; Jirakittayakorn & Wongsawat 2017 Front Neurosci 11:365; Sci Rep 2020 https://www.nature.com/articles/s41598-020-63980-y

### Gamma: Lutz 2004, 40 Hz claims, lucid-dream stimulation

- **Evidence level:** moderate
- **Finding:** Lutz, Greischar, Rawlings, Ricard & Davidson (PNAS 2004) recorded 8 long-term Tibetan Buddhist practitioners vs 10 controls and found self-induced high-amplitude gamma (25–42 Hz) synchrony during non-referential compassion meditation — a finding about trained meditators, not about listening to 40 Hz. Voss et al. (Nature Neuroscience 2014) applied frontal tACS during REM: 25 Hz and 40 Hz (not 2, 6, 12, 70, 100 Hz) increased dream lucidity/self-awareness — electrical stimulation, single study. '40 Hz = enlightenment' is marketing.
- **Usable parameters:** Lucidity cue: 60–90 s of 40 Hz (or 25 Hz) isochronic/AM pulse every ~8 min during the deep/'dream' segment; closing section 25–40 Hz AM for 'clarity'. Gamma via binaural is unreliable — use amplitude modulation.
- **Notes:** Moderate for the lab findings; none for 'enlightenment'.
- **Source:** Lutz et al. 2004 PNAS 101(46):16369–73; Voss et al. 2014 Nat Neurosci 17:810–812; Garcia-Argibay 2019

### Light-and-sound devices: Dreamachine, Grey Walter, Mind Alive, Kasina, Spatial Angle Modulation

- **Evidence level:** moderate
- **Finding:** Brion Gysin & Ian Sommerville built the Dreamachine in 1959–60 (letter 15 Feb 1960; demonstrated to Ginsberg Apr 1961): a slotted cylinder on a 78-rpm turntable around a 100 W bulb giving 8–13 flashes/s (alpha), inspired by W. Grey Walter's 'The Living Brain' (1953) and his 1940s strobe/EEG work (photic driving). Commercial AVE: Mind Alive (Dave Siever, Edmonton; DAVID devices, 1980s→) with sessions typically 1–40 Hz (e.g., 7.83, 10, 14 Hz SMR, ~1–4 Hz 'dissociation'); MindPlace Procyon (2007) and Kasina (2013) decode AudioStrobe/SpectraStrobe light tracks. The Monroe Institute's Spatial Angle Modulation (patent application US 2013/0010967, Atwater & Turner, filed 2011) moves a perceived point-source around the listener instead of relying on a <30 Hz frequency difference. Photosensitive epilepsy risk peaks at ~15–25 Hz flicker.
- **Usable parameters:** If a light track is offered: 8–13 Hz eyes-closed flicker only, never 15–25 Hz; audio-only SAM analogue: rotate a tone around the head at 0.1–0.5 rev/s (azimuth panning + slight Doppler), tremolo-style variant at 4–8 Hz.
- **Notes:** Photic driving of EEG is real (moderate); 'states' claims anecdotal. Mind Alive/Kasina specifics from memory; verify.
- **Source:** Wikipedia 'Dreamachine', 'Mind machine'; ZKM/Arthur magazine (Geiger 2003); Google Patents US20130010967A1; Monroe Institute UK SAM explainer https://www.monroeinstituteuk.org/spatial-angle-modulation/

### Hobbyist preset lore: 6.3 Hz 'astral projection', 4 Hz lucid, 963/936 Hz 'pineal'

- **Evidence level:** none (no evidence)
- **Finding:** The frequency-meaning tables that power YouTube/app presets descend from late-1990s/2000s hobbyist compilations (notably the 'Brainwave Frequency Listing' hosted at lunarsight.com, compiled from tagged secondary sources), BrainWave Generator (Noromaa Solutions, Finland, 1998) user presets, SBaGen (Jim Peters, open-source, 'drop' sequences), and I-Doser (2005; 30–45 min 'doses' such as 'Astral Projection', 'Hand of God', 'Gates of Hades'; 2010 'digital drugs' panic). Typical entries: 6.3 Hz 'mental & astral projection, accelerated learning'; 4.5 Hz 'shamanic/dream state'; 3.5 Hz 'feeling of unity'; 2.5 Hz 'endogenous opiates'; 1.0 Hz 'well-being/HGH'; 0.5 Hz 'Thompson epsilon'; 33 Hz 'Christ consciousness/pyramid frequency'; 63 & 70 Hz 'astral projection'; '963 Hz pineal/God frequency' (Solfeggio) and '936 Hz pineal decalcification' (a 2010s YouTube video title). No origin traces to any study; several entries are circular citations of each other.
- **Usable parameters:** If the guide wants the lore numbers: 6.3 Hz theta (on ~200 Hz) for the 'projection' window; 4.5 Hz 'shamanic'; 3.5 Hz 'unity'; 963 Hz only as a faint overtone; 33 Hz as isochronic pulse. Preset durations 30–45 min; 'drop' shape = 12→4 Hz over 15–30 min then hold.
- **Notes:** Marking these 'none' lets the guide quote them while saying plainly they are folklore.
- **Source:** lunarsight.com/freq.htm (503 at fetch time); Music From the Firmament '6.3 Hz Astral Projection' Bandcamp; Spotify 'Astral Projection Theta 6.3–7.0 Hz'; the3rdstate.net guide; Wikipedia 'Mind machine' (I-Doser panic)

### What listeners actually report (phenomenology to set expectations)

- **Evidence level:** moderate
- **Finding:** Consistent anecdotal reports from Hemi-Sync, Holosync, I-Doser and YouTube users: heaviness/limb dissolution at 10–20 min; 'vibrations', buzzing, racing-heart sensations, floating, auditory 'roaring', hypnagogic imagery and voices at 20–35 min in theta; time distortion and 'click-outs' (gaps in continuity) in delta; occasional sleep-paralysis fear; most people simply fall asleep. These match the laboratory description of hypnagogia (Hori's EEG stages; alpha dropout → theta ripples → vertex sharp waves) and sleep-paralysis/vestibular OBE research (Blanke; Cheyne), all explainable without 'travel'. Expectation and setting drive most reported intensity.
- **Usable parameters:** Timeline for the guide: 0–5 settle; 5–15 heaviness (alpha→theta); 15–35 imagery/'vibrations' (6–4 Hz); 35–50 'no time' (≤3 Hz); 50–60 return. Build the 'vibration' narration and spatial sweep into 20–35 min.
- **Notes:** Moderate that these sensations occur; none that they constitute inter-dimensional or time travel.
- **Source:** Monroe 1971; CIA memo 'click-out'; practitioner guides (SINE, sleeplull, the3rdstate); hypnagogia literature (Hori et al. 1994; Cheyne 2003 sleep paralysis; Blanke & Mohr 2005 OBE) — latter from memory

### Joe Dispenza — pineal gland breath (Becoming Supernatural, 2017)

- **Evidence level:** none (no evidence)
- **Finding:** Dispenza's 'pineal breath' (ch. 5, pp. 140–142): sit upright, contract perineum, then lower and upper abdomen, inhale through the nose 'drawing energy up the spine' to the crown, hold the breath ~10 s with muscles engaged, repeat 3–10 rounds; he claims the CSF pressure 'squeezes' piezoelectric calcite micro-crystals in the pineal so it 'shimmers' and becomes an antenna for 'frequencies beyond the senses', and that his workshop EEGs show extreme gamma. Mechanism is not supported by neuroscience (pineal calcification exists; no piezoelectric 'antenna' function is established). He does not publish binaural beat settings; his meditations use composed music (Barry Goldstein).
- **Usable parameters:** Optional seated pre-track breath block: 3–5 rounds of [contract pelvic floor+abdomen → 5 s inhale up the spine → 8–10 s hold → release], total ≤ 3 min, BEFORE lying down; never during the hypnagogic section.
- **Notes:** Breath-holds with muscle locks raise blood pressure — exclude for hypertension/pregnancy/glaucoma.
- **Source:** drjoedispenza.com 'A Practical Guide to Chasing the Mystical'; Dispenza, Becoming Supernatural (Hay House 2017); summaries (blas.com, meditate.sg)

### Manifestation systems: Neville Goddard SATS, Joseph Murphy, ThetaHealing, Abraham-Hicks 17 seconds

- **Evidence level:** traditional (a lineage's own teaching, not a tested claim)
- **Finding:** Neville Goddard ('Feeling Is the Secret', 1944; lectures to 1972) prescribes the 'state akin to sleep' (SATS): lie immobile, drowsy but conscious, imagine a brief first-person scene implying the wish already fulfilled (e.g., a friend congratulating you), loop it with sensory detail, and 'sleep in the feeling of the wish fulfilled'; practice at the same time daily. Joseph Murphy ('The Power of Your Subconscious Mind', 1963) similarly uses the 'sleepy state' and repetition of a single word. Vianna Stibal's ThetaHealing (trademarked 1995) claims practitioners 'go up to the Seventh Plane' in a 4–7 Hz theta state; a controlled EEG study failed to find increased theta. Abraham-Hicks (Esther Hicks, channeling since the 1980s) claims 17 s of 'pure thought' starts attraction momentum and 68 s (4×17) is a 'combustion point'. None has empirical support; SATS maps onto hypnagogia, which the track can supply.
- **Usable parameters:** SATS/manifestation window: 6–8 min at 6–7 Hz with narration silence (or only a soft drone) for the listener's own looped scene; optional 68-s timed 'pure thought' block marked by a soft bell; place at ~minute 15–23 (theta onset) and again at the return (~minute 50).
- **Notes:** 'Traditional' = defined practice with a lineage; evidence for manifestation effects: none.
- **Source:** Goddard 1944 'Feeling Is the Secret'; mynevillegoddard.com/techniques/sats; McGill OSS on ThetaHealing https://www.mcgill.ca/oss/article/pseudoscience/thetahealingr-money-youll-spend-never-existed ; Wikipedia 'ThetaHealing'; Abraham-Hicks workshop lore (secondary)

### Pineal gland, DMT and 'decalcification' claims

- **Evidence level:** weak
- **Finding:** Rick Strassman ('DMT: The Spirit Molecule', 2001) hypothesized the pineal releases DMT at birth, death and in mystical states — explicitly a hypothesis. Barker et al. 2013 (with Borjigin and Strassman) found trace DMT in rat pineal microdialysate; Dean et al. 2019 (Sci Rep) found DMT-synthesizing enzymes and DMT at serotonin-comparable levels in rat cortex and pineal, and a surge at cardiac arrest in rats; pineal removal did not abolish it; human relevance unknown. Pineal calcification (corpora arenacea) is real and age-related; no evidence that sound (963/936 Hz or any beat) alters it or 'releases DMT'.
- **Usable parameters:** None. The guide should state that 'pineal activation/decalcification' and 'DMT release' are claims without evidence; the pineal's established role is melatonin/circadian timing (darkness + evening listening is the only evidence-based 'pineal' lever).
- **Notes:** 'Weak' refers to the endogenous-DMT science (real but of unknown significance); the audio claims are none.
- **Source:** Michigan Medicine news on Dean et al. 2019 https://www.michiganmedicine.org/health-lab/mystical-psychedelic-compound-found-normal-brains ; Aeon 'Could the brain produce its own psychedelic compound'; Wikipedia 'Rick Strassman'; St John 2016 'The DMT Gland' IJSNR 7(2)

### Dolores Cannon QHHT

- **Evidence level:** none (no evidence)
- **Finding:** Cannon began hypnosis with her husband in 1968 (past-life regression after a client spontaneously regressed), resumed late 1970s, and developed Quantum Healing Hypnosis Technique: single sessions of ~4–6 hours aiming at the 'somnambulistic' (deepest) trance level, which she claimed only ~5% of people enter spontaneously, to contact 'The Subconscious' (SC) for healing and information; critics (Carroll) note no medical training and 'nothing quantum about it'.
- **Usable parameters:** Only the framing: deep-trance work in this lineage is multi-hour; a 45–60 min track should be presented as an 'entry' not an equivalent.
- **Notes:** Past-life/ET material is channeled narrative.
- **Source:** Wikipedia 'Dolores Cannon'; QHHT official descriptions (session length/somnambulism from memory; verify)

### Wim Hof Method (breathing) — effects and fatal risks

- **Evidence level:** moderate
- **Finding:** WHM = cold exposure + breathing + commitment; the breathing is ~30–40 deep breaths followed by exhalation and breath-hold to urge, then a 15-s recovery inhale-hold, 3–4 rounds. Kox et al. 2014 (PNAS, 12 trained vs 12 controls, endotoxin challenge) showed elevated epinephrine and reduced pro-inflammatory cytokines; a 2024 meta-analysis of 8 studies rated evidence quality 'very low'. The hyperventilation can cause syncope; ≥32 deaths (mostly drownings/shallow-water blackout) have been reported as of 2024.
- **Usable parameters:** Do NOT embed WHM-style hyperventilation in a lying-down hypnagogic track; if referenced, instruct seated practice beforehand, never in water, never while driving.
- **Notes:** Moderate for acute sympathetic/anti-inflammatory effect; none for 'cosmic alignment'.
- **Source:** Wikipedia 'Wim Hof' (Kox 2014 PNAS 111:7379; 2024 meta-analysis; death reports)

### Hans Cousto — The Cosmic Octave (1978) with planetary tones and computed EEG octaves

- **Evidence level:** none (no evidence)
- **Finding:** Cousto (Swiss mathematician/musicologist) formulated the Cosmic Octave in 1978 (Die Oktave, 1984; English 'The Cosmic Octave', LifeRhythm 1988; 'Relating Sound to Color…' 1980): any period T becomes an audible tone by f = (1/T)·2^n. Verified values: Earth day 194.18 Hz (n=24, G), Earth year 136.10 Hz (n=32, C#, 'Om' tone, also Indian sadja lore via Berendt), Platonic year 172.06 Hz (F), synodic Moon 210.42 Hz (n=29, G#), Sun 126.22 Hz (B; NOT an orbital period — Cousto derives it from a different constant; verify), Mercury 141.27, Venus 221.23, Mars 144.72, Jupiter 183.58, Saturn 147.85, Uranus 207.36, Neptune 211.44, Pluto 140.25 Hz. Independent recomputation from modern periods reproduces all within 0.3 Hz (Pluto 140.52 with the modern period). EEG-band octaves (halving): Earth year 136.10 → 8.506 / 4.253 / 2.127 / 1.063 Hz; Earth day 194.18 → 12.136 / 6.068 / 3.034 / 1.517; Platonic 172.06 → 10.754 / 5.377 / 2.688 / 1.344; Moon syn. 210.42 → 13.151 / 6.576 / 3.288 / 1.644; Sun 126.22 → 15.78 / 7.889 / 3.944 / 1.972; Mercury 141.27 → 8.829 / 4.415 / 2.207; Venus 221.23 → 13.827 / 6.913 / 3.457 / 1.728; Mars 144.72 → 9.045 / 4.523 / 2.261; Jupiter 183.58 → 11.474 / 5.737 / 2.868 / 1.434; Saturn 147.85 → 9.241 / 4.620 / 2.310; Uranus 207.36 → 12.96 / 6.48 / 3.24 / 1.62; Neptune 211.44 → 13.215 / 6.608 / 3.304 / 1.652; Pluto 140.25 → 8.766 / 4.383 / 2.191. Note Sun/16 = 7.889 Hz ≈ Schumann 7.83 (coincidence exploited by marketers). Any biological effect of 'planetary' tones: no evidence.
- **Usable parameters:** Drone set: 136.10 Hz (Om/Earth year) as the central drone; 194.18 (Earth day) and 172.06 (Platonic) as alternates; beat targets derived by octave: 8.51 Hz (Earth-year alpha), 4.25 Hz (Earth-year theta), 2.13 Hz (delta), 6.07 Hz (Earth-day theta), 1.34 Hz (Platonic delta). Tempo (if any): 136.10/2^? → ~63.8 BPM (Earth year), 91.0 BPM (Earth day).
- **Notes:** The arithmetic is exact (strong); the 'none' rating is for claimed effects. Sun tone derivation needs checking against the book.
- **Source:** Planetware history page https://www.planetware.de/octave/music-and-everything.html ; Planetware tuning-fork booklet PDF; Cousto, The Cosmic Octave (1988); recomputation this run (Python, tropical year 365.2422 d, synodic month 29.530589 d, sidereal periods)

### Othmer, Spotts and 'deep delta' lineages (from memory; search budget exhausted)

- **Evidence level:** weak
- **Finding:** Siegfried & Sue Othmer (EEG Institute) developed infra-low-frequency neurofeedback (ILF, down to ~0.1 mHz) — a clinical feedback method, not auditory entrainment, often cited by vendors to legitimize 'epsilon' audio. Dane Spotts (Zygon International, late 1980s–90s) marketed the 'Mind Lab'/'SuperMind' light-sound units and 'Ultra Meditation' tapes with 'delta' programs. Neither publishes entrainment frequencies that transfer to audio.
- **Usable parameters:** None beyond the general delta/epsilon segment already specified.
- **Notes:** Included for completeness of the lineage list; low confidence on Spotts specifics.
- **Source:** EEG Institute (eeginfo.com) ILF literature; Zygon catalogs (1990s) — verify

### Hemi-Sync-style headphone/EM caveat and digital-drug panic

- **Evidence level:** moderate
- **Finding:** Beyond the 2002 air-conduction finding, the 2010 Oklahoma 'digital drugs'/I-Doser scare and the FDA's Class-III classification of medical-claim AVS devices show the regulatory posture: entrainment audio is sold as 'wellness'; medical claims trigger enforcement.
- **Usable parameters:** Label the track as a meditation/relaxation aid, not a medical device; avoid therapeutic claims in the guide.
- **Notes:** Regulatory, not efficacy, point.
- **Source:** Wikipedia 'Mind machine' (regulation section); Wikipedia 'Hemi-Sync' (Stone 2002)

### Design recommendations from this lens

- Honor the psychoacoustic limits that are actually established: carriers 100–400 Hz (sweet spot 200–300; Monroe's patent says 275 Hz), beat frequencies ≤ 30 Hz, level-matched L/R, headphones mandatory; implement anything ≥ 30 Hz ('gamma', 'lambda') as isochronic/amplitude-modulated pulses, not binaural.
- Use Monroe's patented 'septon' stacking for a thicker, more robust beat: left {200, 204, 208} / right {204, 208, 212} Hz (4 Hz spacing) gives simultaneous binaural and monaural 4 Hz; add a phased pink-noise bed at least 10 dB below the beats with a ~6 s phase cycle.
- Adopt the Gateway preamble literally (it is the lineage the user is invoking): Energy Conversion Box (60 s) → Resonant Tuning hum on a choir drone (2–3 min, drone at 136.10 Hz 'Om'/Earth-year or C 256) → Affirmation (45 s) → REBAL crown→feet→crown visualization (60 s, paired with a 5–6 s head-to-toe spatial sweep) → count 1–10 into Focus 10.
- Session ladder (≈60 min): 0–5 preamble; 5–15 Focus 10 descend 10→7 Hz (≤0.5 Hz/min); 15–18 hold 7.83/7.5 Hz ('transfer window'); 18–26 Focus 12 at 4.25–5 Hz with a 6–8 min SATS/manifestation silence window (6–7 Hz works too); 26–38 'vibration' section at 4→3 Hz with slow 0.2 Hz spatial sweep and rising modulation depth; 38–47 Focus 15 'no time' at 2.1–3 Hz with a 5-min epsilon dip to 0.25–0.5 Hz; 47–52 Focus 21 at 1.0–1.5 Hz with 60–90 s 40 Hz AM 'lucidity cues' every ~8 min; 52–58 return via Awakened-Mind stack (1.5 + 5.5 + 10 + 15 Hz, amplitudes 0/-3/-6/-12 dB); 58–60 beta wake-up 16 Hz on 400/416 Hz (Monroe's wake recipe).
- 15-minute daily version: 2 min preamble (hum + affirmation), 5 min ramp 10→6 Hz, 6 min hold at 6.07 Hz (Earth-day theta octave) or 6.3 Hz (preset lore) with a 68-s 'pure thought' block, 2 min return to 12 Hz.
- Choose drones from systems with internal consistency rather than mixing inharmonic sets: either Cousto (136.10 central, 194.18/172.06 colors) or a C=256 just-intonation ladder (256/288/320/341.3/384/426.7/480 for the 7-chakra section à la Goldman/Beaulieu/Hero); reserve Solfeggio tones (396…963) for a brief ascending overtone shimmer or omit them.
- Derive beat targets from the drone's octaves so the guide can state the math honestly (e.g., 136.10 Hz → 8.51/4.25/2.13/1.06 Hz; 194.18 → 12.14/6.07/3.03/1.52 Hz) — this gives 'planetary' coherence without pretending it has physiological evidence.
- Aesthetics from the New Age canon that users recognise as 'deep': no tempo or drum pulse, 30–90 s harmonic drift, long reverb tails, zither/bell upper partials, ascending key centers through the piece (Halpern), slow rotating 'vortex' panning of a vocal/toning layer (Kenyon; Monroe SAM analogue at 0.1–0.5 rev/s).
- Include optional eyes-closed light-flicker guidance only at 8–13 Hz (Dreamachine range) and never 15–25 Hz; keep any 'pineal breath' or breath-hold block seated and BEFORE lying down, ≤3 min.
- In the guide, tag each element with the evidence level recorded here, and explicitly state that Focus-level frequencies are reconstructions (TMI has never published them), Solfeggio tones are a 1999 numerology, 432 Hz's Nazi/Rockefeller story is false, Tesla's quotes are apocryphal, and no sound alters the pineal or releases DMT.

### Cautions from this lens

- Nothing in this lens supports the requested outcomes (inter-dimensional travel, time travel, control of 'vibration/energy/frequency', 'inhuman' cognition). The CIA memo is a 1983 speculative staff synthesis with no experiments; SRI/Stargate was closed in 1995 after review found no operational value. The guide must not promise 'immediate, undoubted' results; typical honest expectation is relaxation, hypnagogic imagery, vibrations/floating sensations and time distortion after 20–40 min, highly dependent on expectation and setting.
- Binaural-beat EEG entrainment itself is unproven (Ingendoh 2023: 5 for, 8 against, 1 mixed); subjective anxiolytic/relaxation effects are small (Garcia-Argibay 2019). A 2002 UVA finding that Hemi-Sync EEG effects vanished with air-conduction headphones raises the possibility of transducer artifacts.
- Medical/psychiatric exclusions: epilepsy or photosensitivity (especially if any flicker is added; 15–25 Hz is most provocative), psychosis/dissociative disorders (deep-trance and OBE-style content can destabilize), PTSD with sleep-paralysis history, cardiac arrhythmia/pacemaker (no electrical devices), hypertension/pregnancy/glaucoma (breath-holds), hearing conditions (keep SPL moderate, e.g., ≤ 70 dB).
- Never combine with driving, operating machinery, bathing/swimming, or Wim-Hof-style hyperventilation lying down or in water (≥32 reported deaths from WHM-style breathing, mostly drowning/shallow-water blackout).
- Sleep paralysis, 'vibrations', 'click-outs' and vivid hypnagogic imagery can be frightening; include a 'how to come back' instruction (move fingers/toes, open eyes, sit up) and an explicit statement that these are normal hypnagogic phenomena.
- Do not market as a medical device or make therapeutic claims (FTC/FDA precedent: Clark 2004 judgment; AVS devices with medical claims classified Class III). Describe it as a meditation/relaxation aid.
- Several numbers circulating online are folklore with no origin (6.3 Hz 'astral', 33 Hz 'Christ consciousness', 936 Hz 'pineal decalcification', 111/222/333/444 'angel' tones); if quoted, label them as lore.
- Items flagged 'from memory; verify' (Fehmi, Beaulieu fork specs, Mind Alive/Kasina defaults, Spotts, Cannon session length, Beck 3.92 Hz, AIR 1995 details, Akimoto 2018, Cousto's Sun-tone derivation, Gateway Wave exercise lists beyond Wave III) were not re-verified because the web-search budget was exhausted; a follow-up turn can confirm them.

### Skeptic fact-check of this lens

*Overall reliability:* High for the historical and bibliographic core: the Monroe patents (1975, 1993, 1994), the CIA Gateway memo numbers, the SAM patent, Tesla's 787,412, Lakhovsky's US 1,962,565, Ingendoh 2023, the Wim Hof death count, the Clark FTC action, Stargate's 1995 AIR review and the TMI 1978/1983/1985 dates all verified verbatim against primary or cited sources, and the lens correctly brands Solfeggio, 432-Hz, Tesla quotes, 3-6-9 and chakra tables as modern. Errors found are mostly bibliographic: the Gateway Wave IV exercise list is wrong (Intro F15 belongs to Wave V; no 'Null Point'), the Garcia-Argibay meta-analysis is cited with the wrong volume (83, not 73), the Radin–Emoto study is misdescribed as 432-Hz evidence, the 'FDA Class III' point is a court finding not a classification, and a few memo paraphrases (kundalini 4–7 Hz) and figure-caption details are slightly firmer than the text supports. Items the dossier itself flagged 'from memory' (Cousto's Sun-tone derivation, Beck 3.92 Hz, Thompson 1988, Dreamachine letter date) remain unverified because the shared web-search budget was exhausted; WebFetch of primary pages was used instead. Evidence-level ratings are appropriate and, if anything, generous to the psychoacoustic 'strong' items. Main gap is coverage: the lens omits the foundational binaural-beat trials (Lane 1998 etc.), the ASSR/monaural literature, the 40-Hz GENUS and Persinger lineages, and the Hermetic/Theosophical origins of 'vibration/frequency' and pineal talk that the user's request is actually built on.

*Items the lens missed, added by the skeptic:*

- Lane, Kasian, Owens & Marsh 1998 (Physiology & Behavior 63:249–252): the foundational controlled binaural-beat study (beta 16/24 Hz vs theta/delta 1.5/4 Hz; vigilance and mood) — the root of every 'beta beats improve attention' claim; plus Padmanabhan, Hildreth & Laws 2005 (Anaesthesia 60:874–877, pre-op anxiety), Wahbeh et al. 2007 (JACM 13:25–32, 7 Hz theta beats), Le Scouarnec et al. 2001, and Chaieb et al. 2015 (Front Psychiatry) review.
- Monaural beats and auditory steady-state responses: Pratt et al. 2010 (Hearing Research) show monaural beats evoke stronger cortical SSR than binaural; Schwarz & Taylor 2005 (40 Hz ASSR) — the real reason AM/isochronic carriers are preferable for gamma; Oster 1973 himself noted monaural beats are perceptually stronger.
- Will & Berg 2007 (Neuroscience Letters 424:55–60) EEG synchronization to periodic clicks/drums at 1–8 Hz — the only lab basis for isochronic/drum 'entrainment'; Neher 1961/1962 (Human Biology 34:151) 'auditory driving' by drumming at 3–8 beats/s and Rouget's (Music and Trance, 1985) critique; Maxfield 1990 (4–4.5 Hz drumming EEG); Harner's core-shamanism drumming at 4–7 bps (~205–220 BPM, 'The Way of the Shaman' 1980) as the modern consciousness-tech tempo standard.
- The 40 Hz 'GENUS' lineage that actually drives modern 40 Hz hype: Llinás & Ribary 1991 (PNAS, 40 Hz thalamocortical binding = 'consciousness frequency' origin); Iaccarino et al. 2016 (Nature, 40 Hz light in AD-model mice); Martorell et al. 2019 (Cell, 40 Hz auditory clicks); Cognito Therapeutics human trials — none involve binaural beats.
- Persinger's 'God Helmet'/Koren helmet (1980s–2000s, weak temporal-lobe magnetic fields, 'sensed presence') and the failed double-blind replication by Granqvist et al. 2005 (Neuroscience Letters 379:1–6) — a major consciousness-technology lineage absent from the lens.
- Historical roots of the user's own vocabulary: The Kybalion (1908, 'Principle of Vibration'), W.W. Atkinson 'Thought Vibration or the Law of Attraction in the Thought World' (1906), Blavatsky's 'Secret Doctrine' (1888) placing the 'third eye' in the pineal, Descartes (1649) pineal 'seat of the soul', Leadbeater 'The Chakras' (1927) and Christopher Hills 'Nuclear Evolution' (1977) as the origin of rainbow chakra-colour/note tables — needed to date all chakra-frequency tables as 20th-century inventions.
- Bruce Tainio's 'healthy body frequency 62–72 MHz, disease begins at 58 MHz' factoid (c. 1992 'BT3 Frequency Monitor', Tainio Technology/Eastern Washington) — the single most-quoted 'body frequency' number online, with no instrument validation; and David Hawkins' 'Map of Consciousness' (Power vs Force, 1995; log 'calibrations' 20–1000 via applied kinesiology), routinely conflated with 'frequency'.
- Marko Rodin's 'Vortex-Based Mathematics' (1990s–2000s) as the true origin of 3-6-9 numerology later attributed to Tesla; and the 2014 'Schumann resonance has risen to 8.5/13/36 Hz' hoax (misread Tomsk/Russian Space Observing System spectrograms) that underlies most 'Earth is ascending in frequency' content.
- Two evidence-based 'vibration' levers relevant to the hum/resonant-tuning segment: resonance-frequency breathing at ~0.1 Hz (~6 breaths/min; Lehrer, Vaschillo et al. 2000/2003; basis of HeartMath 'coherence') and humming raising nasal nitric oxide ~15-fold (Weitzberg & Lundberg 2002, Am J Respir Crit Care Med 166:144–145); Bhramari pranayama EEG (Vialatte et al. 2009).
- Monroe-lineage specifics: the residential Gateway Voyage (1979→) vs the home-study Gateway Experience; 'Metamusic'; Atwater's 1997 paper 'Accessing anomalous states of consciousness with a binaural beat technology' (J. Scientific Exploration 11(3):263–274) — the only Monroe-affiliated quasi-scientific exposition of the Focus levels; Atwater's Stargate role (1977–87) linking the two lineages.
- Hypnagogia/OBE science to pin the 'from memory' citations: Mavromatis 'Hypnagogia' (1987); Hori, Hayashi & Morikawa 1994 (9 EEG stages); Blanke et al. 2002 (Nature 419:269, TPJ stimulation induces OBE); Cheyne 2003; Nelson et al. 2006 (Neurology, REM intrusion and NDE/OBE); Ehrsson 2007 (Science) and Lenggenhager 2007 — the experimental basis for 'OBE without travel'.
- Flicker-light 'pineal/DMT' machines currently popular: Lucia N°03 (Austria, 2009), Ajna Light, Roxiva; flicker-induced hallucination research (ffytche 2008; Pearson et al. 2016) — the modern successors of the Dreamachine, with the same 15–25 Hz seizure caveat.
- Cymatics (Hans Jenny, 'Kymatik' 1967; Chladni 1787) — the visual rhetoric behind 'frequency shapes matter' in sound-healing marketing; Alfred Tomatis and the 'Mozart effect' (Rauscher et al. 1993; Pietschnig et al. 2010 meta-analysis null).
- John C. Lilly (isolation tank 1954; 'Programming and Metaprogramming' 1968; 'The Center of the Cyclone' 1972) and Itzhak Bentov's 'Stalking the Wild Pendulum' (1977) heart–aorta ~7 Hz standing wave (the source of the memo's 7 Hz) with Sannella's 'physio-kundalini' (1976) — the immediate intellectual neighbours of Monroe that the memo synthesizes.
- 528 Hz citation chain: Lee Lorenzen's 'clustered water' and Glen Rein's 1998 DNA-absorption/chant experiments are the sub-sources of Horowitz's 'DNA repair' claim; and the 'scientific/philosophical pitch' C=256 history (Sauveur 1713; Chladni; Verdi 1884; LaRouche 1988) that is the common root of the Goldman/Beaulieu/Hero/Renold systems.
- Open-source preset ecosystem: Gnaural (2006, GPL; its schedule format is the lingua franca of hobbyist beat ladders) and the bwgen.com preset library (1998→) — the actual provenance of most '6.3 Hz astral' tables, which the dossier attributes mainly to lunarsight.

## 5. Audio engineering for a long-form entrainment track

*Lens scope:* Audio engineering and sound design for a long-form (45-60 min) binaural-beat / entrainment track, synthesised procedurally in Python/numpy at 48 kHz, delivered as 24-bit FLAC and 320 kbps MP3 (plus a 12-15 min daily version)

### Binaural carrier sweet spot

- **Evidence level:** **strong**
- **Finding:** Binaural beats are perceived most strongly with carriers of roughly 300-500 Hz (Oster: best near 440 Hz; Licklider/Webster/Hedlun: largest audible beat ~35 Hz occurs at 350-500 Hz carriers; Schwarz & Taylor: the 40 Hz binaural-beat ASSR was only measurable at a 400 Hz mean carrier). Detection is best for beat rates of 10-15 Hz and reaches 50% detection at ~30 Hz (Perrott & Nelson 1969).
- **Usable parameters:** Primary (perceptual workhorse) carrier 400-440 Hz (e.g. 432 Hz or 408.3 Hz = 3 x 136.1); beat rates 0.5-30 Hz only; keep every binaural carrier <= 500 Hz; research carriers that produced measured effects: 250 Hz (Jirakittayakorn), 340 Hz (Reedijk), ~400 Hz (Schwarz & Taylor).
- **Notes:** Citation details taken from the project dossier (docs/RESEARCH_DOSSIER.md, verified there against PubMed/PMC); WebSearch budget was exhausted this turn so they were not re-searched.
- **Source:** Oster G (1973) Sci Am 229(4):94-102, PMID 4727697; Licklider, Webster & Hedlun (1950) JASA 22:468-473; Perrott & Nelson (1969) JASA 46:1477-1481 doi 10.1121/1.1911890; Schwarz & Taylor (2005) Clin Neurophysiol 116:658-668 PMID 15721080; Grose, Buss & Hall (2012) Hear Res 285:40-45 https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC3299837/

### Why carriers above ~1 kHz fail

- **Evidence level:** **strong**
- **Finding:** Binaural beats depend on interaural phase comparison in the superior olivary complex; neural phase-locking to the fine structure degrades above ~1-1.5 kHz, so the beat percept fades above ~1000 Hz and disappears by ~1500 Hz. Grose & Mamo found no binaural-beat response at 810/850 Hz carriers in most listeners, and Schwarz & Taylor found no ASSR above 3 kHz, whereas acoustic (monaural) beats worked at both low and high carriers.
- **Usable parameters:** Hard ceiling 1000 Hz for any binaural pair; practical ceiling 500 Hz; anything you want above 800 Hz (shimmer, bells, 963/852 Hz 'Solfeggio' tones) must be a monaural/AM or plain drone layer, never a binaural pair.
- **Source:** Oster 1973; Licklider et al. 1950; Grose & Mamo 2012 (cited in Grose, Buss & Hall 2012 PMC3299837); Schwarz & Taylor 2005 PMID 15721080

### Low carriers (136.1 Hz) and critical-band spacing of multiple carriers

- **Evidence level:** moderate
- **Finding:** Beats are heard with carriers down to ~100 Hz, but at low carriers the percept is weaker and consumer headphones roll off. Carriers are processed in separate auditory filters when separated by more than one ERB; ERB (Glasberg & Moore 1990) computed here: 39 Hz at 136.1 Hz, 54 Hz at 272.2 Hz, 68 Hz at 400 Hz, 71 Hz at 432 Hz. Stacked beats on carriers an octave or more apart therefore do not cancel; they are separate beat percepts that sum to a composite 'flutter'.
- **Usable parameters:** Use 136.1 Hz as the symbolic/Om carrier at -3 to -6 dB relative, with 272.2 Hz and 408.3 or 432 Hz as the main beat carriers; keep carriers >= 1 ERB apart (>= 40-70 Hz, ideally octave/fifth relations); no more than 2-3 distinct beat rates at once; carriers within 6 dB of each other.
- **Source:** Licklider et al. 1950; Glasberg & Moore (1990) Hear Res 47:103-138 ERB formula ERB = 24.7(4.37 f/kHz + 1); ERB values computed in this session

### Multiple simultaneous carrier pairs and stacked beat rates (Monroe patent)

- **Evidence level:** weak
- **Finding:** Monroe's US 5,213,562 describes using up to 100 separate carrier pairs (or one broadband pair) to produce many beat frequencies at once that replicate an EEG waveform, reports FFR simultaneously at 1.5, 4 and 6 Hz (Fig 3B), 2, 4, 7 Hz (3D), 0.5, 3, 4 Hz (3F), names 275 Hz as the 'most effective harmonic carrier', gives the worked example 100 Hz vs 104 Hz = 4 Hz beat, and states beats below ~30 Hz are entrainment-producing. It also claims 'phased pink sound' at >= 10 dB below the beat signals enhances FFR by up to 30%.
- **Usable parameters:** Stack at most three beat rates on three carriers, e.g. 136.1/272.2/408.3 Hz; pink-noise bed at -10 to -25 dB relative to carriers; example 'EEG-replica' stacks from the patent: {1.5, 4, 6 Hz}, {2, 4, 7 Hz}, {0.5, 3, 4 Hz}.
- **Notes:** Patent claims are not peer-reviewed evidence. The patent does NOT contain the phrase 'mind awake body asleep' nor an explicit delta+beta combination; that pairing is Monroe Institute practitioner lore (Focus 10), not the patent.
- **Source:** US Patent 5,213,562 (Monroe, 1993) https://patents.google.com/patent/US5213562A/en (fetched and read this session)

### Two ways to offset multiple carriers and the intra-ear distortion products they create

- **Evidence level:** moderate
- **Finding:** If the left ear has harmonically related carriers (136.1, 272.2) and every right-ear carrier is shifted by the same beat offset (140.1, 276.2), the right ear's own spectrum becomes inharmonic and the cubic difference tone 2f1 - f2 = 2(140.1) - 276.2 = 4.0 Hz is an acoustic (monaural) beat at exactly the beat rate, which reinforces rather than cancels. If instead the right-ear carriers are scaled by a common ratio (right = left x (1 + 4/136.1)), the beats stack as 4 Hz on 136 Hz and 8 Hz on 272 Hz (theta + alpha simultaneously). Either is legitimate; mixing the two schemes produces unintended sub-Hz wobble.
- **Usable parameters:** Scheme A (same beat on all carriers): L = {136.1, 272.2, 408.3}, R = L + f_beat. Scheme B (scaled): R = L x (1 + f_beat/136.1) giving beats f_beat, 2 f_beat, 3 f_beat. Never use opposite-sign offsets on different carriers (beat images lateralise in opposite directions).
- **Source:** Combination-tone psychoacoustics (Plomp 1965 JASA 37:1110; Goldstein 1967 JASA 41:676); arithmetic computed in this session

### Interaural level and processing of the binaural pair

- **Evidence level:** moderate
- **Finding:** Oster reported the beat strength is roughly independent of the relative intensities of the two tones, but Gu et al. 1995 showed beats are NOT heard when one ear's tone is below threshold, and any interaural level difference lateralises the carrier image and reduces the centred beat percept. The pair must stay identical in level, dry, centred, and must not pass through any stereo-unlinked processing.
- **Usable parameters:** ILD = 0.00 dB (same float amplitude in both channels); both ears >= 30 dB above the noise bed in the carrier's band; no reverb, panning, Haas delay, width or per-channel limiting on the binaural bus; stereo-linked gain only; verify narrowband RMS of each carrier differs by < 0.1 dB between channels.
- **Source:** Oster 1973; Gu, Y. et al. (1995) 'Failure to hear binaural beats below threshold' (cited in dossier); engineering practice

### Time course of the EEG response: implications for plateau length

- **Evidence level:** moderate
- **Finding:** With a 250 Hz / 256 Hz pair (6 Hz beat) for 30 min, Jirakittayakorn & Wongsawat found no significant theta increase at 5 min, a peak at 10 min (18 of 19 electrode positions), 12 positions at 15 min, 4 at 20 min, 8 at 25 min, 1 at 30 min, and nothing post-stimulus. The effect appears between 5 and 10 min and then habituates.
- **Usable parameters:** Minimum useful plateau 10 min; prefer plateaus of 10-15 min, then change something (beat rate, carrier, added layer) to counter habituation; a 5-min stage is too short to expect any measured effect.
- **Source:** Jirakittayakorn N, Wongsawat Y (2017) Front Neurosci 11:365 https://www.frontiersin.org/articles/10.3389/fnins.2017.00365/full (fetched this session)

### Entrainment induction ramp rates

- **Evidence level:** anecdotal
- **Finding:** Standard entrainment practice (SBaGen, Gnaural, Brainwave Generator presets, Monroe/Atwater session design) starts near resting alpha (~10 Hz) and glides down slowly; no controlled study fixes the rate, but practitioner convention is <= 1 Hz per minute descending and 1-2 Hz per minute on the re-alerting ascent, with linear or raised-cosine (smoothstep) frequency trajectories so the beat never jumps.
- **Usable parameters:** Induction: 10 -> 8 Hz over 4-6 min (0.33-0.5 Hz/min), 8 -> 6 Hz over 4 min (0.5 Hz/min), 6 -> 4 Hz over 4-6 min; deep: 4 -> 3 Hz over 5 min; return: 3 -> 8 Hz over 5 min (1 Hz/min), 8 -> 13 Hz over 4 min (1.25 Hz/min); implement ramps as smoothstep in f_beat(t), not step changes.
- **Source:** SBaGen sequence documentation (Jim Peters, uazu.net/sbagen); Gnaural (gnaural.sourceforge.net) schedule editor; Atwater FH (1997) 'Accessing anomalous states of consciousness with a binaural beat technology' J Sci Exploration 11(3):263-274

### Safe re-alerting ending (SMR)

- **Evidence level:** traditional (a lineage's own teaching, not a tested claim)
- **Finding:** Ending a session in the sensorimotor-rhythm band (12-15 Hz, Sterman) for a few minutes is the practitioner standard to avoid post-session grogginess; a sleep variant omits the return and ends in delta with a slow fade.
- **Usable parameters:** Final 3-5 min at 12-14 Hz beat (e.g. 13 Hz), then a 20-30 s raised-cosine fade to silence; for the sleep variant hold 2-3 Hz and fade over 60 s.
- **Source:** Sterman MB, Friar L (1972) EEG Clin Neurophysiol 33:89-95 (SMR definition); Monroe Institute / Hemi-Sync 'return to full waking consciousness' convention; Siever D, Mind Alive AVE session design

### 'Mind awake / body asleep' stacking (Hemi-Sync Focus 10 style)

- **Evidence level:** weak
- **Finding:** Monroe Institute practitioners describe Focus 10 as a delta/theta beat (body) stacked with a beta beat (mind) on separate carrier pairs. This pairing is not in the patent and has no controlled evidence; it is implementable because the beats sit in separate auditory filters.
- **Usable parameters:** Stage example: 2 Hz beat on 136.1 Hz (-3 dB) + 4 Hz on 272.2 Hz (0 dB) + 15 Hz on 408.3 Hz (-6 dB) for 8-10 min; keep the beta pair quieter so the composite flutter stays gentle.
- **Source:** Atwater FH (1997) J Sci Exploration 11(3):263-274; Monroe Institute Gateway Experience descriptions; US 5,213,562 (confirms multi-beat stacking only)

### Monaural beats are a physiologically stronger stimulus than binaural beats

- **Evidence level:** **strong**
