Jirakittayakorn & Wongsawat 2017 Int J Psychophysiol 120:96-107 (40 Hz binaural beat, 20 min, working-memory recall); Ross & Lopez 2020 Sci Rep 10:7002; Chan et al. 2022 PLoS ONE 17:e0278412 (1 ms clicks every 25 ms; 68-78 dB; safe over 1 h/day) and Martorell et al. 2019 Cell 177:256-271 (mouse data; no dementia claims imported); Lutz, Greischar, Rawlings, Ricard & Davidson 2004 PNAS 101:16369-16373 (25-42 Hz gamma in long-term practitioners: a correlate of training, not of a tone); Cade & Coxhead 1979 The Awakened Mind; Wise 1995 The High-Performance Mind (pattern described qualitatively; no independent replication); Tomatis, The Conscious Ear (1991) and the unverified Benedictine-abbey story - 'air' shelf labelled anecdotal |
| Stage 8 - Return: Sankalpa Repeated, Schumann Second Mode (SMR close) | 51–55 | Same three carriers (136.10 / 272.20 / 408.30 Hz, R = L + f_beat). 14.3 Hz on 408.3 Hz replaces Monroe's patented 400/416 Hz wake-up pair with an in-system equivalent. | 10 → 14.3 | Binaural schedule: 10.0 -> 14.3 Hz over 51:00-53:30 (1.7 Hz/min); HOLD 14.3 Hz 53:30-54:30; 54:30-55:00 30 s raised-cosine fade of the entire mix to silence; 10 s of digital silence closes the file.; 40 Hz AM fades out 50:40-51:00; isochronic layer resumes on 204.15 Hz at pulse rate = f_beat, -9 dB, until the final fade.; Optional narration (-10 dB): 51:10-52:00 sankalpa repeated 3x (listener silently); 52:00-53:30 externalisation cues (sounds in the room, weight of the body, fingers and toes), ending with a counted 1-to-10 ascent in the Monroe manner, one count per 6 s.; Bonsho strike at 53:30 (137.8 / 301.8 / 378.6 Hz, peak -6 dB, T60 45 s so its tail is what the fade carries out); tingsha pair at 54:20 (-14 dB).; Tanpura -8 dB until the fade; overtone drone and chamber layers silent; bowls silent.; Noise bed -25 dB with breath pacer quickening to a 5 s cycle (12/min) by 53:30, +/-2 dB.; Call-back pattern: none (already completed); the counted ascent and bell serve as the externalisation marker. | Satyananda, Yoga Nidra stages 7 (sankalpa repeated) and 8 (externalisation); Sterman & Friar 1972 EEG Clin Neurophysiol 33:89-95 (SMR 12-15 Hz); Lane et al. 1998 (16 and 24 Hz beats: more correct detections, better mood); Schumann second mode ~14.1-14.3 Hz (symbolic; 'Earth-tuning' unproven); Monroe US 5,356,368 (400/416 Hz 'beta' wake-up; voice inserts at -10 dB) - adapted to the 408.3 Hz carrier; Hemi-Sync Listening Tips (count down/up, splash cool water if spacey); Jirakittayakorn & Wongsawat 2017 (effects vanish post-stimulus; 10 min most effective) - reason the close is short and alerting; Harvard Health 'Blue light has a dark side' - the only evidence-based 'pineal' advice (dim/red light after an evening session; daylight after a morning one), placed in the guide |

*Global layers:* BINAURAL BUS (runs 0:40-54:30 continuously): left carriers 136.10 / 272.20 / 408.30 Hz, right = left + f_beat(t) (Scheme A, same sign on all three, never ratio-scaled, never opposite-sign); levels 408.3 = 0 dB (reference ~-20 dBFS RMS per ear), 272.2 = -3 dB (-6 dB in Stage 7), 136.1 = -3 dB; float64 phase accumulators, smoothstep f_beat(t) ramps (10 -> 8.51 -> 7.83 hold -> 6.07 -> 4.5 -> 4.25 hold -> 3.0 hold -> 10 hold -> 14.3), max descending slope 0.52 Hz/min, max ascending 1.7 Hz/min, plateau lengths 3, 7, 5, 7 and 1 min with a timbral change at every plateau; dry, centred, ILD 0.00 dB, no reverb, no panning, no per-channel processing, no amplitude crossfades (only phase-continuous frequency ramps); 5 s raised-cosine fade-in, 30 s fade-out.; ACOUSTIC-BEAT LAYER on 204.15 Hz (3:2 above 136.1), identical in both channels, -9 dB (-12 dB under the drum, -10 dB in the Bardo): monaural beat (204.15 + 204.15+f_beat) in Stages 2-3a, Hann-gated 50 %-duty isochronic pulses at rate f_beat from 12:00 through Stage 6 and Stage 8, and 40 Hz sinusoidal AM (depth 60 %) in Stage 7; gate period recomputed per pulse from instantaneous f_beat. Speaker-compatible redundancy and the stronger ASSR driver.; TANPURA / OM DRONE: strings 204.15 / 272.20 / 272.20 / 136.10 Hz (Pa-Sa-Sa-Sa), pluck every 2.5 s, 48 harmonics at 1/n, jivari Gaussian window sweeping harmonic index 25 -> 6 over 6 s per pluck (+12 dB, width 4 harmonics), two unison copies detuned +/-0.15 % with +/-0.05 % random walk; -8 dB nominal, -12 dB under the drum, -14 dB in the intention window, -10 dB in the imaginal window; reverb wet -12 dB.; PINK-NOISE BED: Kellet pk3 pink, -25 dB re binaural pair (never above -20, down to -31 in silent windows), 0.7 shared + 0.3 independent per channel (IACC ~0.7), -6 dB notch 340-476 Hz, high-passed at 60 Hz; carries the BREATH PACER as a +/-2 to +/-3 dB raised-cosine swell (rise:fall 45:55) with a 2 -> 6 kHz low-pass sweep on the rise: 4 s cycle (15/min) 0-3 min, 4 -> 6 s by 7:00, 6 -> 10 s (6/min, 0.1 Hz) by 9:00, held 9:00-39:00, 10 -> 8 s by 47:00, 8 -> 5 s (12/min) by 53:30; suspended during the thunder climax. (The 0.1 Hz swell is also what any 'epsilon' labelling refers to.); SINGING-BOWL BUS (reverb T60 3.6 s, wet -15 dB): additive mode pairs, ratios 1 : 2.83 : 5.42 : 8.77 : 12.87 : 17.7, fundamentals pinned exactly to 136.10 / 204.15 / 272.20 Hz, (2,0) doublet split = min(f_beat, 7) Hz slewed over 2 s, T60 30/15/8/4/3/2 s, strikes every 20-40 s where present, rim-singing swells 30-60 s with 0.6-1.2 Hz tremolo depth 20-30 %.; BELL MARKERS: bonsho strike (137.8 / 301.8 / 378.6 Hz, T60 45 s, 2-4 Hz AM) at 0:06, 7:00, 16:00, 24:00 (bowl), 31:00, 39:00, 47:00 and 53:30 - every stage boundary; tingsha pairs (2.8 kHz detuned 10 Hz) as accents; all on the reverb bus.; LOW DRONE REGISTER (dry, centred): 68.05 Hz (C#2 = 136.1/2) didgeridoo/Gyuto hybrid in Stages 4-5 and the kargyraa option in Stage 7; 5th (340.25 Hz) and 10th (680.5 Hz) harmonics +10 dB; formant sweeps 1-2 kHz; never below 55 Hz as a tone; master high-pass 25 Hz.; VOICE-SYNTH BUS (glottal pulse + two formant resonators, slow rotation pan 0.02-0.03 Hz, reverb wet -15 dB): hum guide (Stage 2), So'ham cue, A-OM-HUM (Stage 5), seven-vowel ladder and IAO (Stage 6), sygyt overtone drone (Stage 7, dry); +3 dB 3 kHz high shelf only in Stage 7. All vocal material is synthesised: no sampled icaros, joiks, yuwipi, Yeibichai, Midewiwin, Gyuto or Yolngu recordings (owned ceremonial property).; PERCUSSION BUS: modal frame drum (f0 72 Hz) at 4.5 -> 4.25 Hz in Stage 4 and the call-back (4 x 7 at 1.75 Hz / 30 s at 7.5 Hz / 4 x 7) at 39:00; +/-0.35 pan alternation; reverb wet -15 to -18 dB; sigma 4 ms jitter.; REVERB BUS: synthetic stereo IR (exponentially decaying Gaussian noise, independent L/R, T60 3.6 s, pre-delay 18 ms, HF T60 = LF/3 with damping corner 3.8 kHz, early taps 11/17/23/31 ms at -6 to -12 dB) for bowls, bells, horn, conch, voices, drum; a second 'stone chamber' IR (T60 4.5 s) for the 110/70 Hz drone in Stage 5; 0 % on the binaural, isochronic/AM and noise buses.; OPTIONAL NARRATION BUS (-10 dB, dry, centred, low-passed 8 kHz): calibration instruction, hum/intention cues, rotation of consciousness, Bardo recognition cue, call-back announcement, sankalpa repeat, counted ascent. Rendered as a separate stem so a voiceless version ships alongside.; MASTER CHAIN: sum in float64 -> 4th-order Butterworth SOS high-pass at 25 Hz -> single scalar gain to -18 LUFS integrated -> 4x-oversampled true-peak check (<= -1.5 dBTP; soft safety limiter at -1 dBFS that should touch 0 samples) -> 30 s final fade -> TPDF dither only if a 16-bit derivative is made.

*Mastering:* Sample rate 48 kHz throughout (render, master, deliverables); internal float64, mix bus float64, stored float32. Loudness: integrated -18 LUFS (acceptable -20 to -16; daily version -17 LUFS), short-term never above -12 LUFS-S (the thunder passage is the test), loudness range 6-10 LU (do not exceed 11 so platform normalisers do not switch to dynamic mode). True peak <= -1.5 dBTP measured on 4x-oversampled signal per ITU-R BS.1770-4 (gives margin for the MP3 encode; Spotify asks <= -1 dBTP, -2 dBTP if louder than -14 LUFS); a soft-knee safety limiter at -1 dBFS (5 ms look-ahead, 150 ms release, stereo-linked) should log zero touched samples. Gain is a single scalar computed from the numpy BS.1770 measurement (K-weighting: high-shelf + RLB high-pass re-derived for 48 kHz; 400 ms blocks, 75 % overlap; -70 LUFS absolute gate, -10 LU relative gate), never ffmpeg loudnorm in dynamic mode; if loudnorm is used as a check, run two-pass with linear=true, force -ar 48000, and confirm 'Normalization Type: Linear'. High-pass 25 Hz (4th-order Butterworth SOS); no content below 25 Hz; lowest intentional tone 68.05 Hz; nothing above 8 kHz louder than -30 dBFS (tinnitus/hyperacusis). Binaural-bus integrity: ILD < 0.1 dB per carrier, no reverb/panning/width/crossfeed, track is intentionally not mono-compatible; verify the beat on the decoded MP3. A 5 s 1 kHz sine at -20 dBFS opens both versions as a volume-calibration tone (set to a comfortable speaking level, then leave the volume; target 55-70 dBA, never above 75 dBA for the hour; WHO/ITU-T H.870 80 dBA for 40 h/week; a -18 LUFS master at 50-60 % phone volume sits well inside this). Deliverables: (1) FLAC 24-bit 48 kHz: ffmpeg -i master.wav -c:a flac -compression_level 8 -sample_fmt s32 out.flac, verify ffprobe bits_per_raw_sample=24 (fall back to flac --best --bps 24 if not); expected ~12.4 MB/min, ~680 MB for 55 min, ~187 MB for 15 min; (2) MP3 320 kbps CBR simple stereo: ffmpeg -i master.wav -c:a libmp3lame -b:a 320k -joint_stereo 0 -compression_level 0 -ar 48000 -id3v2_version 3 out.mp3 (2.4 MB/min: ~132 MB / 36 MB); no VBR flag together with -b:a; (3) stems: narration-free mix, narrated mix, and the 'sleep ending' alternate (Stage 5 held at 3.0 Hz then a 60 s fade, no call-back, no 40 Hz, no SMR). Dither: none for 24-bit (optional 1 LSB TPDF); TPDF +/-1 LSB without noise shaping only if a 16-bit WAV/CD derivative is produced. Metadata: title, stage cue points as chapter markers at 0/3/7/16/31/39/47/51 min, and the disclaimer text in the comment field. Verification artefacts shipped with the files: per-stage LUFS/true-peak/ILD/DC table, Hilbert beat trace vs schedule plot, envelope-PSD peaks, decimated spectrogram PNG, and the post-MP3 beat re-check.

*Daily short version:* Axis Mundi Daily (15:00, -17 LUFS, same carriers 136.1 / 272.2 / 408.3 Hz, Scheme A, same buses). 0:00-0:05 calibration tone 1 kHz -20 dBFS; 0:06 bonsho strike (137.8/301.8/378.6 Hz). 0:10-2:00 THRESHOLD AND HUM: binaural fades in at 0:10 at 10.0 Hz and ramps to 8.51 Hz by 2:00 (0.8 Hz/min); tanpura 204.15/272.2/272.2/136.1 Hz at -8 dB; hum-guide drone 136.1 + 272.2 Hz at -8 dB with the Bhramari protocol (inhale 4 s, hum 10-12 s, 5 rounds, 0:20-1:30); pink bed -25 dB with a 4 s breath swell slowing to 6 s; 1:30-2:00 intention/sankalpa window (bed -31 dB, tanpura -14 dB, 3 silent repetitions). 2:00-5:00 DESCENT: beat 8.51 -> 6.07 Hz (0.81 Hz/min); monaural beat on 204.15 Hz enters at -9 dB at 2:00 and switches to Hann-gated isochronic at 4:00; bowls at 272.2 Hz (doublet split = min(f_beat, 7) Hz) struck every 30 s; two nada cues panned +0.2 right (tingsha at 2:30, conch 306.19 Hz at 3:45); breath swell 6 -> 10 s (6/min) by 4:00, +/-3 dB. 5:00-11:00 PLATEAU at 6.07 Hz (Earth-day theta octave; 6 min, inside the 5-10 min window where theta rose in Jirakittayakorn 2017 and the 5-min blocks that lowered state anxiety in Chaieb 2017): choose one of two renders - (a) Still: tanpura -8 dB, bowl rim-singing swells at 6:00 and 9:00, 68.05 Hz drone at -10 dB, with a 68 s imaginal/sankalpa window 7:30-8:38 marked by two soft 272.2 Hz bowl tones (bed -28 dB, tanpura -10 dB; the 68 s figure is Abraham-Hicks lore and labelled so); or (b) Journey: the beat holds at 4.5 Hz instead (ramp 6.07 -> 4.5 over 4:00-5:00 at 1.6 Hz/min is too steep, so the Journey render ramps 8.51 -> 4.5 over 2:00-5:00 at 1.34 Hz/min - acceptable only because it is a short descent - then holds), frame drum f0 72 Hz at 4.5 Hz unaccented at -7 dB for 5:00-10:30, 68.05 Hz didgeridoo/Gyuto drone at -9 dB, tanpura -12 dB, and the drum slows 4.5 -> 3.9 Hz while fading 10:30-11:00. 11:00-11:50 RETURN SIGNAL: Still render = bonsho strike at 11:00 plus a 15 s bowl rim swell; Journey render = call-back 4 x 7 beats at 1.75 Hz / 20 s at 7.5 Hz / 4 x 7 (shortened), -6 dB. 11:00-14:00 ASCENT: beat 6.07 (or 4.5) -> 13.0 Hz over 11:00-14:00 (2.3 Hz/min Still, 2.8 Hz/min Journey; the faster ascent is the re-alerting side and still smoothstep and phase-continuous); seven-vowel ladder compressed to 20 s per vowel (136.1, 153.11, 170.13, 181.47, 204.15, 229.67, 255.19 Hz) 11:20-13:40 at -10 dB with IAO at 272.2 Hz 13:40-14:00; isochronic on 204.15 Hz at -9 dB following f_beat; breath swell 10 -> 5 s. 14:00-14:40 HOLD 13.0 Hz (SMR) with sankalpa repeated (optional narration -10 dB), bonsho at 14:05; 14:40-15:00 20 s raised-cosine fade to silence, 5 s digital silence. Use: once or twice daily (cap 90 min/day of any version), morning default, eyes closed, headphones, not while driving; journal within 2 minutes of the end. Honest expectation: calm, heaviness, slower sense of time within 5-10 minutes; nothing more is promised.

### Judge: engineer

- **Design 0: 7/10.** Strengths: Most complete Monroe-lineage architecture (box, resonant tuning, affirmation, REBAL, count-in, Focus 10/12/15/21, counted return) with every stage tied to a specific citation and the fact-check corrections visibly applied (Wave V/VI exercise placement, Lavallee labelled, Jirakittayakorn mood caveat, 4x7 callback labelled convention). Correct psychoacoustic frame: 408.3 Hz primary carrier in the 400-440 Hz sweet spot, all carriers <= 500 Hz, same-sign offsets, phase-continuous smoothstep ramps, no processing on the binaural bus, descent <= 0.5 Hz/min, ascent ~1.4-1.7 Hz/min, redundancy via monaural AM + isochronic, ends in SMR on silence, separate sleep render, calibration tone, exhaustive verification (Hilbert phase-difference, envelope PSD, ILD, MP3 re-check) and a correct mastering chain (scalar gain to -18 LUFS, -1.5 dBTP, 25 Hz HP, joint-stereo off). Chunked float64 render plan is right. Weaknesses: Over-dense: 9 stages, ~12 narration inserts, septon, AM, isochronic, tanpura, bowls, bonsho, Gyuto voice, pad sweeps, chamber drone, vowel ladder, click train, four-rate 'awakened mind' stack. The binaural percept is only as salient as a shallow AM (Grose 2012) and this mix keeps burying it under tonal material that sits inside the carriers' auditory filters. Eleven minutes at <= 2.13 Hz (incl. 1.5 Hz) will be heard as a slowly rotating image and will put most listeners to sleep right before the 40 Hz 'lucidity' cue, which at 90 s and -12 dB cannot do anything (evidence needs 15-20 min). Focus-level frequencies are admitted reconstructions, so the ladder's precision is cosmetic. The 15/min (4 s) breath swell at the start is an audible 0.25 Hz tremolo with no purpose. Drum block is 12-13 min, not the stated 13.5, barely meeting Maxfield's 13-15 min. Technical errors: (1) Septon (200 / 200+f / 200+2f, 200+3f) sits in the same ERB as the isochronic carrier 204.15 Hz and the tanpura Pa string 204.15 Hz, generating spurious ~4 Hz and ~0.35 Hz sub-Hz wobbles the design itself forbids; only the 340.25 AM layer was muted. Septon also yields monaural 2f/3f (9, 13.5 Hz) beats during a 4.5 Hz plateau. (2) Tanpura harmonics land exactly on 272.2 and 408.3 Hz in both ears: coherent summation in the left ear vs a beat in the right ear makes the carrier-band ILD non-zero and confounds the < 0.1 dB ILD check; same for the Stage 2 hum drone at 136.1/272.2. (3) Bowl fundamentals 207.7/277.2 Hz beat at 3.55/5 Hz against the 204.15 isochronic carrier and 272.2 binaural carrier in both ears, an uncontrolled extra rate during 7.83 Hz and 2.13 Hz holds ('within 10 Hz so they do not beat harshly' is backwards: within 10 Hz is exactly where they beat). (4) Gyuto 5th harmonic (340.25 Hz, +10 dB) coincides with the 340.25 Hz monaural AM carrier and reduces/phase-randomises its modulation depth in the 'vibration' stage. (5) 40 Hz click train (lines at 280/400 Hz) over a 1.5 Hz binaural pair: contradictory stimulus classes, 8 Hz monaural beats against 272/408 carriers, 90 s dose meaningless. (6) 408.3 -> 400 Hz carrier glide while the tanpura's 408.3 harmonic is still sounding creates a sweeping 0-8 Hz monaural beat on top of the 14.3 Hz target. (7) 110 + 70 Hz chamber drone is ~1 ERB apart (roughness). (8) Pad bus is specified as 'identical L/R, no pan' in synthesis notes but given 0.02 Hz azimuth rotation in Stage 6. (9) Drum held at 4.0 Hz while f_beat falls to ~3.4 Hz in Stage 6 breaks the stated ±0.5 Hz lock. (10) Four simultaneous rates (1.5/5.5/8-10/15 Hz) in the return stack exceed the 2-3-rate guidance in the sound-design lens.
- **Design 1: 6/10.** Strengths: Best-researched cultural architecture with correct attributions throughout (Harner 1980 vs FSS tempo range, Swami Rama's 61 points, six-tone Solfeggio origin, Jahn site list corrected, Theosophical pineal genealogy, Chavín values, Bardo recognition instruction). 3.0 Hz floor is the wisest beat-rate decision of the three (avoids the sub-3 Hz rotating-image percept and Lane 1998 delta vigilance/mood costs). Clean beat schedule (10 -> 8.51 -> 7.83 -> 6.07 -> 4.5/4.25 -> 3.0 -> 10 -> 14.3) with plateau-level timbral changes against habituation, a 15-min drum block that meets Maxfield's minimum, the intention window that dips the beds but never the binaural bus, breath-swell suspension during loud events, 12 s pacer option, bowl doublets slewed to f_beat, correct ascent rates, SMR close on silence, sleep ending, narrated and voice-free stems, correct mastering and verification. Weaknesses: Busiest and least comfortable arc: a shofar blast and bonsho in the first 40 s (loudest 40 s of the track apart from the thunder) while the listener is still setting level; bells at every boundary; nada cues; horn; conch cluster; and a -3 dB 'thousand thunders' climax with a 400 ms cut delivered at the 3 Hz floor, precisely the state in which the safety lens documents sleep-paralysis-type fear. The 'skip to 39:00' opt-out is unusable by someone lying masked in hypnagogia. Formal call-back arrives 8 min after the drum stopped, after an unrelated Bardo episode. 40 Hz segment (3m20 at -10 dB) is tokenistic. 4 s breath tremolo at the start as in Design 0. Dense inharmonic events (bonsho, conch, five-colour cluster) repeatedly put roughness-range beating inside the carrier filters, degrading the one thing the track is supposed to deliver. Technical errors: (1) Bowl fundamentals pinned EXACTLY to 136.1/204.15/272.2 Hz with doublet split d = f_beat: each doublet component sits ±f/2 from the carrier, so both ears hear monaural beats at f/2 (and 3f/2 in the right ear) at -6 dB peak, the opposite of the stated 'only the intended wobble, never a clash'. (2) Chavín conch cluster 300.9-311.6 Hz is checked only against 408.3 Hz; it is 29-39 Hz from the 272.2 Hz left carrier and 19-29 Hz from the 282.2 Hz right carrier (ERB 54 Hz), producing three minutes of roughness-band beating at -10 dB; '9:8 above so it does not clash' is wrong, a major second in this register is roughness by definition. (3) Bonsho partials 137.8/301.8/378.6 Hz beat at 1.7 Hz against the 136.1 carrier and ~30 Hz against 272.2/408.3, eight times, with 45 s tails. (4) Stage 3 conch cue 306.19 Hz is 34 Hz from 272.2 (inside its ERB). (5) 40 Hz AM on 204.15 Hz puts a 244 Hz sideband 28 Hz from the 272.2 carrier; dipping that pair 3 dB does not remove the roughness. (6) Hum guide and tanpura at the exact left-ear carrier frequencies create carrier-band ILD and a right-ear monaural beat (same flaw as Design 0). (7) Five-colour cluster includes 204.15 vs 226.83 Hz (22.7 Hz roughness) at -10 dB under thunder peaks at -3 dB. (8) Daily 'Journey' render drums for 5.5 min, far under the 13-15 min minimum it cites. (9) Bonsho at 0:06 overlaps the volume-setting moment.
- **Design 2: 8/10.** Strengths: Cleanest signal design of the three and the only one whose layer table survives an ERB collision check: fixed carriers 250 and 400 Hz (both lab-validated), isochronic reinforcement at 170 Hz, a pure-sine 110 Hz drone with no harmonics, a 600 Hz bowl, noise notched ±1 ERB around both carriers, and the 40 Hz block moved to 380+420 Hz with the 400 Hz binaural pair faded out first so no unintended 10/20/30 Hz beats arise. Beat range confined to 4.2-14 Hz (no rotating-image delta, no vigilance/mood penalty), carriers never move, offsets same-sign, one shared smoothstep schedule, 12-min 6 Hz hold at the exact Jirakittayakorn dose, 13+ min drum locked within ±0.5 Hz of the beat, 40 Hz delivered as monaural + click train at the documented pulse structure for a meaningful 15 min, 0.1 Hz breath pacer on bed/drone only, voice cues short and scheduled from Gingras/Harner/Sharpless, SMR close on silence. Correct ERB arithmetic, correct render/metering/mastering chain, full verification incl. a Stage-5 40 Hz line check and MP3 re-check. Comfort over 60 min is best: sparse, no startles, every event a single bowl. Weaknesses: Sparse to the point of austerity; listeners expecting a 'journey' soundscape may find pure tones + pink noise + one drum thin, and the user's framing (Gateway, manifestation, realms) is served only by a 60 s intention slot and one journey instruction. The 16-min 40 Hz integration block (100%-depth monaural beat + clicks) is the main fatigue risk: 40 Hz AM reads as buzz, and at ~55-60 dB SPL it is also under-dosed relative to the Chan 2022 protocol it cites, so it is long enough to annoy but too quiet to replicate. No sleep render or manifestation/imaginal window is specified as a deliverable. Daily version ascends 6 -> 14 Hz at 4 Hz/min. The +8% every-4th-hit drum accent departs from the unaccented Harner/Maxfield convention. Technical errors: (1) Level arithmetic: two equal components at -15 dB sum to about -12 dB, not the stated -9 dB for the 380+420 Hz pair. (2) Final 3.5 min runs the isochronic pulse at 16 Hz against a 14 Hz binaural beat; they do not cancel, but a 2 Hz interaction between the two pulse streams is audible and breaks the ±0.5 Hz lock rule the design applies everywhere else. (3) Drone level is specified both as '-10 dB float' and 'set by phon (~+10 dB)'; the two are 20 dB apart and must be resolved before build. (4) Chaieb 2017 carrier figures (107-223 Hz) are cited as fact although the fact-check marks them unverifiable. (5) 40 Hz click-train line at 240 Hz sits 10 Hz from the 250 Hz left carrier (coincides with the 10 Hz alpha beat, so benign, but should be noted in the verification expectations). (6) Callback timing: four groups of seven at 2 Hz with 1.5 s gaps is ~18.5 s, not ~14 s (fits the slot anyway). (7) Voice bus at -6 dB, 120 Hz-6 kHz, is louder than the -3 dB carrier pair's partner and sits across both carrier bands during cues; acceptable only because cues are <= 25 s.

*Best:* design 2

*Ideas to graft:*

- From Design 0: the full Monroe preamble shape (energy-conversion box 60 s, hum/resonant tuning against the 110 Hz drone, affirmation slot, REBAL head-to-feet cue, count 1-10 into the descent) and the counted 10-to-1 return; implement the REBAL/vibration sweep as a 5 s low-pass sweep on the noise bed only, never on carriers.
- From Design 0: a 'vibrational state' cue inside the drum block, realised as a rising modulation depth (40% -> 90%) on the 170 Hz isochronic/AM layer plus the one-line 'let them come, smooth them, move a finger if afraid' narration; keeps Monroe's phenomenology without adding a colliding carrier.
- From Design 1: the intention/sankalpa window that drops bed and drone 6 dB for 90 s while the binaural bus continues, and the sankalpa-repeated-3x externalisation at the return; also the 68 s bell-bracketed imaginal window at the end of the 6 Hz hold (labelled lore).
- From Design 1: breath-swell suspension during any loud event and the 12 s (5/min) pacer option for tall/male listeners; the explicit statement that the pacer is a background cue (±1.5 dB) during the journey.
- From Designs 0 and 1: ship a 'sleep' render (stop after the 6 Hz hold, 60 s fade, no 40 Hz, no callback) and a 15-min FSS-style drum-only journey file at 4.2 Hz with callback, alongside the voiced/voice-free 60-min and 15-min renders.
- From Design 1: an optional 2-3 min hold at 7.83 Hz on the way down (labelled symbolic Schumann rate, which is a perfectly good theta beat) to give the Gateway-literate user a named landmark without changing carriers.
- From Design 1: the seven-vowel ascent as a synthesized formant voice during the 10 -> 14 Hz climb, but transposed so every pitch is >= 1 ERB above 410 Hz (e.g. a 600 Hz root ladder: 600/675/750/800/900/1012/1125/1200 Hz, bowl-consonant) and kept at -12 dB, slow 0.02 Hz rotation, reverb on its own bus.
- From Design 0: Monroe-patent 'phased pink sound' (6 s raised-cosine crossfade between two pink streams) as a free aesthetic on the existing bed, and the explicit rule that narration inserts are >= 40 s apart.
- From Design 1: 0.02-0.03 Hz equal-power rotation and a dedicated reverb bus for every non-binaural acoustic event (bowl, drum, any vocal layer), with the stone-chamber IR reserved for a brief 110 Hz passage if one is wanted.
- From Design 0: the explicit equal-loudness (ISO 226) check of the low pair/drone on a headphone target curve and the 3 s RMS rule that no non-binaural layer exceeds the primary carrier except on a strike attack; adopt its per-stage LUFS-S / LRA budget table.

*Must fix before build:*

- Run an automated ERB-collision audit over the final layer table (all carriers, drone harmonics, isochronic sidebands at k*f_pulse, 40 Hz click lines, bowl partials and doublets, any grafted vocal pitches) and reject any tonal component within 1 ERB of 250, 250+f, 400, 400+f Hz; this is what disqualifies the tanpura/bowl/bonsho/conch material of Designs 0 and 1 and must stay true after grafting.
- Fix the 380+420 Hz level arithmetic (each component at -12 dB for a -9 dB pair, or restate as -12 dB) and re-balance the whole Stage 5 so the 40 Hz layers sit under the 250 Hz binaural pair; then shorten or taper the 40 Hz block (10-12 min, AM depth ramped 60-80%, clicks <= -12 dB) and listen-test for buzz fatigue.
- Resolve the drone level spec: state one value, phon-corrected (110 Hz tone matched by ear to a 400 Hz tone at ~60 phon, roughly +8 to +10 dB SPL), and verify on a headphone target curve that both the 250 Hz pair and the 110 Hz drone are audible on consumer earbuds.
- Keep every pulsed layer within ±0.5 Hz of the binaural beat for the whole track: set the final isochronic rate to 14 Hz (or ramp both to 14-15 Hz together) instead of 16 vs 14.
- Add the missing deliverables as named renders: sleep ending (no 40 Hz, no callback, 60 s fade at 6 Hz), voice-free and voiced 60-min, 15-min daily with a gentler ascent (6 -> 14 Hz over >= 3 min), and the optional 15-min drum-only journey.
- Narration hygiene: keep every cue <= 20 s, >= 40 s apart, none during the 6 Hz hold, drum plateau or 40 Hz block except the scheduled ones; lower the voice to -8 dB and notch it -6 dB in the two carrier bands (198-302, 332-468 Hz) so cues do not mask the beat.
- Make the drum journey block strictly unaccented and isochronous (drop the every-4th-hit accent), confirm >= 13 min of continuous drumming before the callback, and label the 4x7 / rapid / 4x7 callback as a core-shamanism convention.
- Verification gates that must pass before any file ships: Hilbert beat trace within ±0.05 Hz of schedule per stage, |L-R| envelope PSD peak >= 20 dB above neighbours, carrier ILD < 0.1 dB (measured with drone/isochronic muted and then unmuted), DC < 1e-4, integrated -18 LUFS via one scalar gain, short-term <= -12 LUFS-S, LRA 6-10 LU, true peak <= -1.5 dBTP with the limiter touching 0 samples, 40 Hz line present only in Stage 5, and all checks repeated on the decoded 320 kbps simple-stereo MP3.
- Fixed RNG seeds per layer and per channel, phase wrap at every block boundary, carried sosfilt/reverb state, 25 Hz 4th-order HP on the master, nothing above 8 kHz louder than -30 dBFS, 5 s 1 kHz -20 dBFS calibration tone followed by >= 5 s silence before any event.
- Guide and metadata: nine-element disclaimer, three-tier stop rules, 18+, headphones-only/no spatial DSP/no bone conduction, no driving for 10-15 min after, the Ingendoh 2023 entrainment caveat, explicit statement that no sound affects the pineal gland (Wahbeh 2007 melatonin null), and 'symbolic' labels on 7.83 Hz, 110 Hz and any Cousto/Solfeggio numbers; remove the unverifiable Chaieb 2017 carrier figures from citations.

### Judge: scholar

- **Design 0: 6/10.** Strengths: Faithful to the lineage the user actually invoked: the Gateway preparatory sequence (Energy Conversion Box, Resonant Tuning hum, full Affirmation with its protective closing line, REBAL, counted 1-10 descent and counted return) is reproduced accurately and attributed to the McDonnell memo / Gateway Experience waves, with the Wave numbering corrected (One-Month Patterning = Wave II, Mission 15 = Wave V, Intro Focus 21 = Wave VI). Focus-level beat values are honestly flagged as third-party reconstructions TMI never published. Monroe's vibrational state is mapped onto Cheyne/Sharpless sleep-paralysis phenomenology with a usable 'slow the out-breath, move a finger' script. The 2.127 Hz / 4.25 Hz / 8.51 Hz values are correctly derived from Cousto's 136.10 Hz and labelled numerological. Harner callback given in the correct Harner 1980 form (4x7 / ~30 s rapid / 4x7) and labelled a core-shamanism convention. Seven-vowel ascent correctly sourced to Demetrius, PGM, Nag Hammadi NHC VI,6. Awakened-Mind stack correctly attributed to Cade/Wise and labelled unreplicated. Gyuto chord voice correctly tied to Smith/Stevens/Tomlinson 1967 with the Hz caveat. Solfeggio omitted with reason. Safety and honesty sections are thorough; sleep render and cue-sheet metadata are thoughtful. Weaknesses: Breadth is narrow for a 'deep dive into all traditions down to the smallest tribes': it is a Monroe/CIA-memo track with Eastern garnish (one Upanishad, HYP Bhramari, Gyuto, bonsho, bowls, Greek vowels) and only Harner for the entire indigenous world; no Africa, Americas beyond Harner's composite, no Sufi, Kabbalah, Taoist, Sikh/Sant Mat inner-sound tradition, no Yoga Nidra/sankalpa frame beyond a 'SATS window'. 'Manifestation' is framed through Neville Goddard and Abraham-Hicks 68-s lore rather than the older sankalpa tradition. 'Cosmic alignment' and 'third eye' are served only by labels (7.83 Hz, Focus 15/21), not by any tradition-specific practice. The journey drum ends at 37:00 but the Harner callback does not sound until 50:00, thirteen minutes and two Focus levels later, which breaks the ritual logic of the callback (it is the signal that ends the drum journey). The 2877.3 Hz 'homage tone' at -42 dB is an in-joke with no function. 963/852 Hz dropped but 7.83/14.3 'Schumann', Thompson epsilon and 110 Hz chamber all kept as symbolic layers, which makes the guide's honesty burden heavier. Very dense layering (up to nine simultaneous buses) in Focus 12 risks masking the subtle binaural percept the design depends on. Technical errors: (1) Monroe septon at L {200,204,208}/R {204,208,212} Hz runs 23:00-33:00 simultaneously with the Hann-gated isochronic layer on 204.15 Hz, so the isochronic carrier sits 0.15 Hz, 4 Hz and 8 Hz from the septon components and generates uncontrolled sub-Hz wobble and extra 4/8 Hz acoustic beats in both ears, exactly the mixed-scheme interference the design elsewhere forbids. (2) Gyuto voice at 68.05 Hz with its 5th harmonic boosted +10 dB lands on 340.25 Hz, the monaural AM carrier running at the same time in Stage 6; with 0.4 % vibrato it produces ~1-1.4 Hz beating against the AM carrier. (3) 110 Hz + 70 Hz chamber drone in Stage 7 is a sustained 40 Hz acoustic beat/roughness (and 42 Hz against the 68.05 Hz voice) in the stage meant to be the deepest and stillest. (4) Tanpura strings at 272.2 Hz and the 3rd harmonic of 136.1 Hz coincide exactly with the binaural carriers in both ears, so part of the 'binaural' beat becomes a right-ear monaural beat; acknowledged only for the 408.3 harmonic, not for the 272.2 string, which is louder. (5) Vowel-ladder ratios 5:4 and 15:8 are just-intonation (Ptolemaic/Zarlino) intervals, not 'Pythagorean/Timaeus' (Timaeus gives 81:64 and 243:128). (6) Bowls at 207.7/277.2 Hz are 3.5-5 Hz from the 204.15 and 272.2 Hz carriers and will beat against them; 'within 10 Hz of the drone' is the problem, not the solution. (7) 136.1 Hz pair held at 2.0 Hz while the other pairs run 7-9 Hz for 10 min: a second simultaneous beat rate in the 'body' pair is Monroe lore, but its sub-3 Hz percept is lateral motion, which conflicts with the stated rule that binaural layers carry no motion. (8) The 'Perrott & Nelson 10-15 Hz most salient' figure is not reproduced, good, but Stage 9 still cites 14.3 Hz as 'Schumann second mode' as if settled (values vary 14.1-14.3 and drift).
- **Design 1: 8/10.** Strengths: By far the broadest and most correctly attributed sourcing: Shinto harae and kotodama, Maimonides on the shofar, bonsho acoustics, Hamsa Upanishad ten sounds with HYP IV.65-102 (right-ear IV.67, loud-to-subtle IV.85-86, deer/snare corrected to IV.94), Vijnana Bhairava v.41, Surya Siddhanta prana arithmetic, Satyananda Yoga Nidra with the rotation point-count corrected (right thumb, ~70 points; 61 points assigned to Swami Rama), Bernardi 2001 for 6/min breathing, Harner 1980 (205-220 bpm) vs FSS ~5 bps vs Gingras 254.7 bpm all stated, Maxfield 13-15 min minimum, Gordon 2026, Rouget's critique, Evenki/Shirokogoroff/Anisimov, Hekhalot seven palaces, Bardo Thodol with Fremantle & Trungpa and the text's own recognition instruction, Bon A-OM-HUM with the Nyingma order noted, Chod imagery without ritual sampling, Jahn/Devereux/Ibison site list corrected (Loughcrew), Hypogeum 114 not 110, dungchen 58 Hz dropped as unverified, Chavin pututus (Cook/Kolar), Tuvan sygyt (Bergevin 2020, Adachi & Yamada), Balinese ombak as the one tradition that engineers a fixed 5-9 Hz beat, 'Tibetan singing bowl' flagged as a 20th-century market category, Solfeggio correctly described as Puleo's six tones with 963 added by vendors. The arc genuinely serves the four intentions in the traditions' own terms: sankalpa and the imaginal window for 'manifestation' (Yoga Nidra, not New Thought first), nada/Bhramari for inner-sound/ajna work, a measured drum journey with explicit instructions and a formal callback for 'journeying', seven-vowel/Pythagorean ascent and Bardo recognition for 'other realms', ending in externalisation. Every chant is synthesised, not sampled; ceremonial property is respected. 3.0 Hz floor, SMR close, opt-out for Bardo imagery, calibration tone and sleep ending show the safety lens was read. Honesty statement in the rationale is complete (Ingendoh 5/8/1, Wahbeh melatonin null, Theosophical pineal genealogy, Kybalion vocabulary, apocryphal Tesla, memo as literature essay). Weaknesses: The breadth risks the 'universal shaman' pastiche the indigenous lens warns against: shofar labelled as 'harae', Shinto present only as a word, Jewish/Tibetan/Greek/Andean/Siberian material stacked in one 55-minute arc with Cousto's numerological 136.1 Hz as the glue; the rationale admits this but the guide must say it more loudly. The Bardo Thodol is a text read to the dying; staging its 'thousand thunders' at -3 dB as a sonic climax for a relaxed listener is a liberty that needs stronger framing and an easier opt-out (the research cautions for trauma, psychosis history, vestibular migraine). The five-colour tone cluster (blue 136.1, yellow 170.13, red 204.15, green 226.83) is the designers' invention, not the text's five Buddha-family lights, and should not be presented as traditional. Callback placed 8 minutes after the drum ends (after the Bardo) departs from Harner's logic; acknowledged, but a short in-stage return cue is missing. Bhramari and So'ham are cued correctly but Sikh/Sant Mat inner-sound current (the closest textual match to 'travel by sound'), Sufi dhikr, Taoist six sounds and Kabbalistic tzeruf are absent despite being in the research; Rouget/Becker caveat is cited but no possession-tradition tempo-arc device (Gnawa/Zar/Vodou kase) is used, which would have been the honest way to borrow an accelerando. Daily 'Journey' render descends at 1.34 Hz/min, above the design's own 0.52 Hz/min ceiling. Technical errors: (1) Singing-bowl fundamentals 'pinned exactly' to 136.10, 204.15 and 272.20 Hz coincide with the binaural carriers and the acoustic-beat carrier: a bowl tone with T60 30 s at -6 dB present identically in both ears collapses the interaural phase relation of the 272.2 pair (right ear hears 272.2 bowl + 272.2+f carrier as a monaural beat, left ear hears a coherent sum) and its doublet split adds d/2 and f±d/2 beats; pinning to carriers is self-defeating, not protective. (2) Chavin conch cluster at 300.9-311.6 Hz sits 29-39 Hz above the 272.2 Hz carrier, inside one ERB (54 Hz at 272 Hz), producing 29-39 Hz roughness against the carrier in both ears; only the distance from 408.3 was checked. (3) 110 Hz + 70 Hz chamber drone is a sustained 40 Hz acoustic beat in the 3 Hz 'stillest' stage (and 42 Hz against the 68.05 Hz horn/voice). (4) Stage 7 40 Hz AM on 204.15 Hz puts sidebands at 164 and 244 Hz, within one ERB of the 136.1 Hz carrier (ERB 39 Hz) as well as the 272.2 Hz carrier; only the latter is acknowledged. (5) Tanpura strings at 272.2 Hz and the 136.1 string's harmonics 2 and 3 coincide with the 272.2 and 408.3 Hz binaural carriers in both ears, turning part of the beat into a right-ear monaural beat; unacknowledged. (6) Vowel-ladder ratios 5:4 and 15:8 cited to 'Plato Timaeus / Pythagorean / Boethius' are just-intonation intervals; Timaeus uses 81:64 and 243:128. (7) 'The one small bowl-EEG study used a 6.68 Hz beat' repeats a figure the fact-check could not verify. (8) Shofar fundamental 306.19 Hz is 34 Hz from the 272.2 carrier (inside its ERB) while the carrier is fading in; brief, but the first thing the listener hears is roughness. (9) Breath pacer 'rise:fall 45:55' at a 4 s cycle gives 1.8/2.2 s, but Stage 1 text says 2 s/2 s.
- **Design 2: 5.5/10.** Strengths: Technically the cleanest of the three: every tonal component is placed at least one ERB from every other (170 Hz isochronic, 250 and 400 Hz carriers, 380/420 Hz gamma pair, harmonic-free 110 Hz drone, 600 Hz bowl), so no unintended beats or roughness arise; the 250/256 Hz lab pair is used at its documented dose; the 40 Hz block is monaural + click train at the Chan 2022 pulse structure; drum locked within 0.5 Hz of the beat and eased before the callback; ascent never leaves the binaural floor of 4 Hz; ends alert at 14 Hz in silence. Attribution is accurate and corrections applied (Grose & Mamo 10/10 at 390/430, Soula 2023, Becher 2015, Harner 205-220 vs FSS 5 bps, Gingras 10/10 vs 2/8 for dreamlike only, Lehrer 4.5-6.5/min range, RESPeRATE evidence for audio pacing). Honesty about entrainment, pineal, Solfeggio and 'travel' is exemplary, and the journey instruction is correctly recognised (Gingras 2014) as the active ingredient. Two renders (voiced/voice-free) and a defensible daily version. Weaknesses: For a judge of comparative religion and ethnomusicology it fails the brief's core request: the user asked for a deep dive into every tradition 'down to the smallest sources and tribes', and this design uses Harner's composite drum, a Bhramari hum, a Monroe count and a Cade/Wise metaphor, nothing else. No Vedic, Tibetan, Taoist, Sufi, Kabbalistic, Greek, Andean, African or Siberian material; no sankalpa/SATS 'manifestation' frame in the full track (only a 60-s 'intention-scene' option in the daily version); no inner-sound (nada/anhad/Saut-e-Sarmad) arc for the 'third eye'; no ascent/descent architecture for 'realms'; no cultural-credit section because there is nothing to credit. The 600 Hz bowl is used as a map cue without the note that 'Tibetan singing bowls' are a modern market category. 15 minutes of 40 Hz AM buzz at the end of a relaxation session is likely to be experienced as harsh even at -9 dB, and its placement over a rising alpha beat serves cognition studies rather than any tradition's 'integration'. The arc is a laboratory protocol with a journey block bolted on; it can honestly deliver relaxation and absorption but not the culturally framed states the user named. Technical errors: (1) Callback arithmetic: four groups of seven beats at 2 Hz with 1.5 s gaps takes ~18.5 s, not '~14 s', so 38:30 + 18.5 + 30 + 18.5 ends at ~39:37, overrunning the stated 39:28/39:30 stage boundary and colliding with the bowl cue. (2) Final 3.5 min decouple the isochronic pulse to 16 Hz while the binaural beat sits at 14 Hz, contradicting the design's own rule that near-rate periodic stimuli must stay within 0.5 Hz; the 2 Hz difference will read as a slow flutter. (3) 'Lakota/FSS return and account convention' misattributes Harner's journey-account practice to Lakota ceremony (yuwipi ends with the room opened and a meal, not an account). (4) 'Chaieb 2017 monaural beats on 107-223 Hz carriers' repeats carrier values the fact-check could not verify. (5) The 72 Hz drum's 2.14 f0 mode (154 Hz) sits 16 Hz from the 170 Hz isochronic carrier; transient, so minor, but unchecked against the design's ERB rule. (6) Bowl strikes at every stage boundary are called 'the listener's map' but the bowl is never introduced in the guide as synthetic/non-Tibetan. (7) Daily version descends 10->6 Hz at 1 Hz/min and ascends 6->14 Hz at 4 Hz/min against the design's own <=1-1.4 Hz/min convention; acknowledged but inconsistent.

*Best:* design 1

*Ideas to graft:*

- From #2: adopt its ERB-clean spectral layout as the engineering rule for #1: keep every sustained tonal component (bowls, conch cluster, AM sidebands, drone harmonics) at least one ERB from every binaural carrier; in practice move bowl fundamentals off the carriers (e.g. a 600 Hz bowl or the measured 187/236/347/428 Hz set, choosing ones >1 ERB from 272.2 and 408.3), give the 110 Hz drone no harmonics and no 70 Hz partner, and place the 40 Hz gamma stimulus as 380+420 Hz summed in both ears with the 408.3 Hz pair muted for that stage.
- From #2: offer the 250/256 Hz lab pair (Jirakittayakorn's exact stimulus) as the second binaural pair in the 6 Hz block instead of 272.2 Hz, so the theta core reproduces the one controlled time-course study and no longer coincides with the tanpura's Sa string.
- From #2: the explicit journey-instruction cue 45 s before the drum starts and the 'the drum is calling you back; retrace your path' line at the callback, plus the two-render (voiced / voice-free) delivery with the same script printed in the guide.
- From #2: drum rate locked within 0.25-0.5 Hz of f_beat and eased 4.25 -> 3.8 Hz for 30 s before the callback; keep the isochronic layer 3 dB under the drum while the drum runs.
- From #0: the Monroe Gateway preamble as an optional 'Monroe path' opening (Energy Conversion Box 60 s, Affirmation kept whole with its protective closing line, REBAL 5.5 s crown-to-feet sweep on the pad bus, counted 1-10 descent) since that is the lineage the user is invoking; attribute to the McDonnell memo components section and Gateway Experience Wave I.
- From #0: the Cheyne/Sharpless-informed vibrational-state script ('let the vibrations come; smooth them; if fear rises, slow the out-breath and move a finger; you can return at any moment') placed at the Bardo floor, and the Focus-level names (10/12/15/21) as an optional map in the guide, flagged as unofficial reconstructions.
- From #0: Monroe's 'phased pink sound' (6 s raised-cosine crossfade between two pink streams) and the Awakened-Mind-style return stack (alpha binaural + theta AM + light-beta isochronic at 0/-3/-6/-12 dB) for the last two minutes before the SMR close, attributed to US 5,356,368 and Cade/Wise respectively.
- From #0: counted 1-10 ascent with one number per 15 s, bonsho strike marking the stack entry, cue-sheet/chapter markers at stage boundaries, and a separate 'sleep ending' render (hold the floor, no callback, no 40 Hz, 60 s fade).
- From #0: the 'Mission 15 Creation and Manifestation' naming for the imaginal window (Gateway Wave V) alongside the sankalpa, so both the yogic and the Monroe 'manifestation' frames are credited in the guide.
- From the research gaps both other designs ignore: add a Sant Mat / Surat Shabd Yoga 'inner sound current' note and a Sikh anhad shabad / dasam duar note to the nada arc in the guide (the closest living textual match to 'travel between realms by sound'), and use a possession-tradition accelerando (Gnawa/Zar/Korean gut style, e.g. a shaker rising 100 -> 160 bpm over 5 min before the drum plateau) as the honest way to borrow a build-and-tip device, credited to Kapchan/Boddy/Mills.

*Must fix before build:*

- Bowl collision: do not pin bowl fundamentals to 136.10 / 204.15 / 272.20 Hz; sustained bowl tones at exactly the binaural carrier (both ears) and the acoustic-beat carrier destroy the interaural phase relation and add d/2 and f +/- d/2 acoustic beats. Move bowls >= 1 ERB from every carrier (e.g. 600 Hz, or 187/236/347/428 Hz measured set filtered for clearance) or drop sustained bowl tones in the carrier bands.
- Chavin conch cluster (300.9-311.6 Hz) is inside one ERB of the 272.2 Hz carrier (29-39 Hz roughness). Transpose the cluster (use the second modes 601-623 Hz only, or shift to a region >= 1 ERB from both 272.2 and 408.3), or mute the 272.2 pair for the 3 min the cluster plays.
- Chamber drone 110 + 70 Hz produces a sustained 40 Hz acoustic beat (and 42 Hz against the 68.05 Hz horn/voice) in the 3 Hz 'stillest' stage. Use 110 Hz alone, or an octave partner (55 Hz), and run an ERB check on every sustained tone in the 55-140 Hz band.
- Tanpura/drone harmonics coincide with the binaural carriers (272.2 string; 136.1 harmonics 2 and 3) in both ears and convert part of the beat into a right-ear monaural beat. Either move the binaural carriers off the drone's harmonic series (250/400 Hz as in design #2, or 256/384 Hz), or attenuate the drone's 2nd/3rd harmonics by >= 15 dB in the carrier bands, and state in the guide that the beat is deliberately delivered both binaurally and acoustically.
- Stage 7 40 Hz AM sidebands (164/244 Hz) fall within one ERB of both the 136.1 and 272.2 Hz carriers; relocate the gamma stimulus (380 + 420 Hz summed, muting the 408.3 pair) or reduce AM depth and mute the 136.1 pair during it.
- Relabel the seven-vowel ladder: 9:8, 4:3, 3:2, 27:16 and 2:1 are Pythagorean/Timaeus; 5:4 and 15:8 are just-intonation (Ptolemy/Zarlino). Either cite correctly or use 81:64 (172.25 Hz) and 243:128 (258.4 Hz) for a true Timaeus scale.
- Callback logic: the Harner callback is the signal that ends the drum journey; sounding it 8 minutes after the drum has stopped (after the Bardo) needs either a short in-stage return cue at 30:30 (e.g. the 4x7 groups alone) plus the full callback at 39:00, or an explicit guide statement that the sequence is an adaptation. Verify the callback arithmetic fits the stage times (28 beats at 1.75 Hz with 1.5 s gaps = ~20.5 s per block).
- Opener framing: do not label the shofar call 'harae'; present it as a synthesised natural-horn call 'in the manner of' the shofar (Rosh Hashanah, Maimonides on awakening), note that it is not a sacred recording, and either give Shinto an actual sonic element (slow falling-cadence monotone cue) or credit it only as the purification concept. Check that the 306.19 Hz horn does not overlap the 272.2 carrier fade-in.
- Bardo climax: cap thunder so that at the calibrated level it stays <= 80 dB SPL and short-term loudness <= -12 LUFS-S, verify true peak after the passage, state in the guide that the five-colour tone mapping is the designers' device (the text's five lights are the Buddha families: white, blue, yellow, red, green) and that the Bardo Thodol is a text for the dying adapted here as imagery, with an explicit skip-to-39:00 opt-out and the trauma/psychosis/vestibular cautions.
- Remove or flag unverified numbers: the bowl-EEG '6.68 Hz / 482.61 Hz' figures, the exact Chavin pututu values (confirm against the Cook et al. 2010 table), any 'Schumann second mode = 14.3 Hz' precision (14.1-14.3, drifting), and Cousto's Sun tone if ever used.
- Guide text must carry, verbatim or in substance: entrainment unproven (Ingendoh 2023 5/8/1); no sound affects the pineal (Wahbeh 2007 melatonin null; ajna-pineal link is Theosophical, Blavatsky 1888/Leadbeater 1927); 136.1 Hz, 7.83 Hz, 110 Hz, epsilon and Focus-level values are practitioner conventions; Solfeggio omitted as 1999 numerology; Tesla quotes apocryphal; the CIA memo is a one-officer essay; 'Tibetan singing bowl' is a modern market category; all vocal and ceremonial material is synthesised and every tradition is credited with its own frame (sankalpa, nada, bardo recognition, journey) rather than as a mechanism.
- Safety items from the safety fact-check not yet in the design text: Cheyne/Sharpless sleep-paralysis script at the floor stage, sensory-restriction (eye mask + headphones) hallucination note, bipolar disorder and sleep-loss contraindication for any WBTB use, specific sleep-disorder and vestibular/Meniere's cautions, headphone-magnet distance cited to the FDA (not Lee 2009), 18+ label, three-tier stop rules, Cheetah House and crisis-line pointers, and the honest expectation line (calm, heaviness, slower time within 5-10 min; ~26 % state-anxiety drop is the best-documented single-session result).
